patippuri
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolinōkkup pārviyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. 
 tanti periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷki
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭu attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷip patittu varukiṟōm.
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum cērttu avaruṭiya maṟivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivanta, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭurikaḷit tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭurip putiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷi naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉivukūrkiṟōm.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamiyuṭivar eṉṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñari iṉṉum peṟṟut taravilli eṉṟu paṉmoḻi
aṟiñar appātturiyarum, kuṟimatiyar tēkkivitta kariyiruḷi nīkka vanta maṟimaliyār vaḻivanta niṟimaliyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravittalivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa ceytikaḷi ikkaḷañciyatti paṭippār aṉivarum karuttil koḷḷa vēṇṭukiṟōm.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam urippaṉa. tamiḻi āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kākkum ariya oḷiñāyiṟākum. vīḻntupaṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻ
moḻiyi mīṭkavallatu ivartam nūlkaḷākum eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟi ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamiyākum.
pāvāṇarit tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum eṉṟa maṉuṇarvōṭu, pāvāṇar koḷkikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷi veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟiyum, citikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putiyalkaḷi tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyi mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻupōtum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻi ñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷi vāṅki payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷi ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉivil kūṟukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.

vāṉmaḻi vaḷaccurappu
āḻattuḷ āḻamāy akaḻntu taṅkam ēṉ eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu? āḻkaṭaluḷ ceṉṟu, uyirip paṇayam vittu muttuk kuḷikkap paṭuvatu ēṉ? pavaḻap pāḷaṅkaḷi eṭuppatu ēṉ? vayiram mutalām maṇikkulaṅkaḷi iṭiyaṟāmal tēṭit tēṭi eṭuppatu ēṉ?
aṇikalap poruḷkaḷ eṉpataṟku maṭṭumā? vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭṭukkum īṭṭutaṟku ariya vippu vaḷamāka iruntu, nāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyal mēmpāṭṭukku aṭimaṇi
yākavum ivi iruppatāl tāṉē!
ivaṟṟiṉum mēlām vippu vaḷamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu! uṇṭu! avi cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ!
kālam kālamāka aḷḷik koṇṭālum vaṟṟā vaḷamāyt tikaḻum aṟivuk karuvūlamām nūlkaḷ!
oṉṟik kaṟṟāl oru nūṟāy ōrāyiramāyp palkip perukat takka peṟaṟkariya tāy nūlkaḷām pēṟaminta nūlkaḷ! cēynūlkaḷi īṉavalla ceḻu nūlkaḷ!
nuṇ māṇ nuḻipulat tāy nūlkaḷāy iruppiṉum! avaṟṟit tāṅkiya tāḷkaḷ, ettakiya pēṇutal uṭiyavi eṉiṉum kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyak kūṭiyavi tāmē!
kalleḻuttē, katir veppālum kaṭaluppālum kāttup pēṇum kaṭappāṭillār kippaṭalālum aḻintu paṭṭami kaṇkūṭu tāṉē!
ēṭṭu varivukaḷ, nīrē neruppē nīṭitta kālamē puṟṟē pōṟṟā maṭamiyē eṉpavaṟṟāl aḻintu paṭṭamikku aḷavu tāṉum uṇṭō? makkaḷukku māṇap periya payaṉām nūlkaḷ, kaṟkum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum, piṟantu perukivarum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum takka vakiyil avarkaḷukkup pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyar vitta paḻantēṭṭeṉṉa annūlkaḷ, mīḷamīḷat taṭṭillāmal kiṭikkac ceyya vēṇṭuvatu nūṟpatippar tavirāk kaṭamiyallavō!
akkaṭamiyi avarkaḷ kālantōṟum kaṭappāṭākak koṇṭu ceyya, āḷum aracum vāḻum aṟivarum arunnuṇiyātal tāṉē kaṭṭāyat tēvi! ivvāṟu patipparum aṟivarum āḷnarum tattam mūccuk kaṭaṉāka, nūṟkoṭi puritalukku mēṟkoṭi illiyām!
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ oṉṟā iraṇṭā? namakku eṭṭātavi pōka eṭṭiya nūlkaḷ 32. kaṭṭuri poḻivu mutalām vakiyāl kiṭṭiya tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ 12. iṉṉum eṭṭāta tokuppum aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟum vara vāyppuṇṭu!
kaṭita varivu pā varivu eṉa mēlum perukavum vāyppuṇṭu!
ivaṟṟi ellām āyiram acciṭṭu avvaḷavil niṉṟu viṭṭāl, tēṭuvārkkut tēṭum poḻutil vāykkum tiruvākat tikaḻak kūṭumō?
ātalāl, cēy nūlkaḷukku mūlamām tāynūlkaḷ, kālam tōṟum vāṉmaḻi vaḷac curappāka veḷippaṭa vēṇṭum kaṭṭāyam uṇarntu kaṭami puriyum vīṟuṭiyār vēṇṭum! mika vēṇṭum!
ittakiya viḻumiya vīṟuṭiyar - iṉamāṉac cemmal - tamiḻp pōrāḷi - tiruttaku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār, tām muntu muḻutuṟak koṇarnta pāvāṇar paṭippukaḷi mīḷamīḷat tamiḻulāk koḷḷa vikkum muṉ muyaṟciyāy, im mīḷpatippi vaḻaṅkukiṟār! tamiḻulakam payaṉ koḷvatāka! payaṉ ceyvatāka!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.



mukavuri
illaṟamākiya nallaṟam pūṇum makkaḷ vāḻkkiyil tirumaṇamē taliciṟanta maṅkala nikaḻcciyātalālum, nīṇṭa kālamākat tamiḻukkum tamiḻaṉukkum iḻukkunērum vaṇṇam āriyamuṟiyil perumpāl tamiḻa maṇaṅkaḷ naṭipeṟṟu varuvatālum, aṇmiyil yāṉ naṭattivitta pala tirumaṇaṅkaḷil yāṉ uṇarnta kuṟiyi niṟittaṟ poruṭṭum, in nūl eḻutappeṟṟatu.
cēlam, 15-5-56	tēvanēyaṉ

tamiḻar tirumaṇam



muṉṉuri
1. vāḻkki nōkkam
iv vulakiṟ piṟanta x›bvāruvarumoru ciṟintaṉumiṉgmāyvāHviru«puvatālum,av iṉgttiṟkuiṉ¿yamiyātuvē©ṭuvatupehruḷātalālum,ap pehruḷpe‰wrceyavēṇṭiyakaṭakaṟamahtalālumimkyilmakkaŸvāḻ¡kikkuṟiknkāḷiṉpaKmpoUSmaṟamum eṉamū‹ṟāfkkuṟittadrmuṉṉēhr.immū‹wDŸS«, aṟam ciṟantatāyum ēṉi yiraṇṭiṟkum potuvāyu miruttalāṉ, aṟavaḻiyil īṭṭiya poruḷikkoṇṭu aṟavaḻiyil iṉpan tuykkavēṇṭu meṉṉum karuttāl, aṟatti muṉvittu mupporuḷiyum aṟam poruḷ iṉpam eṉa māṟṟiyamittaṉar piṉṉōr.
oruvarkku ūṇ, ici, kāṭci mutaliya piṟavaṟṟālum iṉpamuṇṭāku mēṉum, pēraḷavupaṟṟiyum aimpulaṉun taḻuval paṟṟiyum, peṇṇiṉpamē immiyiṟ pēriṉpamākak koḷḷappeṟṟatu.
peṇṇiṟ peruntakka yāvuḷa kaṟpeṉṉun
tiṇmiyuṇ ṭākap peṟiṉ.	(54)
kaṇṭukēṭ ṭuṇṭuyirt tuṟṟaṟiyum aimpulaṉum
oṇṭoṭi kaṇṇē yuḷa.	(111)
eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvaṉāruṅ kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
makkaḷiṭi matattuṟi vaḷarcciyaṭintu vīṭupēṟṟu nampikki ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, māntaṉ kuṟikkōḷ allatu pēṟu aṟamporuḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉa nāṉkāka vakukkap peṟṟatu. in nāṉkum nāṟporuḷ allatu nāṉmāṇ poruḷ eṉappaṭum.
immiyiṉpamākiya peṇṇiṉpamum maṟumiyiṉpamākiya vīṭṭiṉpamum, oppu nōkka vakiyāl, muṟiyē ciṟṟiṉpam pēriṉpam eṉappaṭiṉum, ciṟṟiṉpamē iyaṟkik kēṟṟatum, uṭaṉē nukartaṟ kuriyatum, eḷitāyk kiṭṭuvatum, kaṇkūṭākak kāṇap peṟuvatu māyiruttaliṉ, atuvē perumpālarāl virumpappaṭuvatām.
ītalaṟam tīviṉiviṭ ṭīṭṭalporuḷ eññāṉṟum
kātal maṉiyāḷum kātalaṉum - tītiṉṟip
paṭṭatē iṉpam paraṉiniṉin timmūṉṟum 
viṭṭatē pēriṉpa vīṭu
eṉṉum auviyār ceyyuḷ, nāṟporuḷiyalpiyum naṉkaṉam terivikkum. ic ceyyuḷiṉ mutalaṭiyi, ītalaṟam tīviṉiviṭ ṭīṭṭal poru''ḷeṉṟum eṉṟu cilar tiruttuvar.
cila tuṟaviyar, variyiṟantu peṇṇi veṟuttup peṇṇiṉpattip paḻittiruppar. atu tuṟaviyarkkē kūṟiyateṉṟu illaṟattār poruṭ paṭuttātu viṭṭuviṭal vēṇṭum.  vilimakaḷuṟaviyum neṟitiṟampiya kāma nukarcciyiyum paḻikkalāmēyaṉṟi, peṇṇiṉpantaṉṉiyē paḻittal kūṭātu. aṅṅaṉam paḻippavar, iṟivaṉ ēṟpāṭṭiyum tam peṟṟōr vāḻkkiyiyum paḻippavarēyāvar.
2. vāḻkki vaki
māntaṉ vāḻkki, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam eṉa iru vakittu, maṉivi yōṭu kūṭi illattiliruntu, ataṟkuriya aṟañceytu vāḻum vāḻkki illaṟam; ulakappaṟṟit tuṟantu, ataṟkuriya aṟattōṭu kūṭik kāṭṭil tavañ ceytu vāḻum vāḻkki tuṟavaṟam. atu maṇavāniliyilum toṭaṅkalām; maṇanta niliyilum toṭaṅkalām.
oruvaṉ maṇañ ceyyātu iṟutivari māṇiyāy (piramaccāriyāy) iruppiṉum, avaṉ vāḻkki illaṟattiṉ pāṟpaṭum.
oruvaṉ nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu nīṅkik kāṭṭil vāḻiṉum, maṉiyāḷoṭu kūṭi vāḻiṉ, atu illaṟattiṉ pāṟpaṭuvatē.
oruvaṉ illattiliruntu maṉiyāḷoṭu kūṭi vāḻiṉum, aṟañ ceyyātu iruppiṉ, avaṉ vāḻkki illaṟamākātu veṟumaṉāṉa ilvāḻkkiyām. oruvaṉ tuṟavu mēṟkoṇṭum ataṟkuriya aṟam pūṇāṉāyiṉ, atu īraṟattoṭuṅ kūṭātu tīviṉiyi mikukkum alvāḻkkiyām.
ikkālattil, tuṟavu pūṇṭōr kāṭṭiṟkuc cellātu nāṭṭilum nakarattilum taṅki, tammāliyaṉṟavari potumakkaṭkut toṇṭu ceyvatu, ciṟanta tuṟavākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
3. illaṟac ciṟappu
iṟivaṉ ēṟpāṭṭiṉpaṭi makkaḷulakam iṭiyaṟātu toṭarntu varuvataṟku illaṟamē kāraṇamātalālum, tuṟaviyarkkum avar muṟṟat tuṟakkumvari iṉṟiyamiyāt tuṇiyāyiruppatu illaṟattārē yātalālum, illaṟattālum vīṭupēṟu kiṭṭumātalālum, māyamāla naṭippiṟkiṭam tuṟavaṟattiṉum illaṟattiṟ kuṟitalālum, illaṟamē nallaṟamām. itaṉālaṉṟō,
illaṟa mallatu nallaṟa maṉṟu
eṉa auviyārum,
aṟaṉeṉap paṭṭatē yilvāḻkki yaḵtum
piṟaṉpaḻippa tillāyiṉ naṉṟu,	(49)
aṟattāṟṟiṉ ilvāḻkki yāṟṟiṟ puṟattāṟṟiṟ
pōoyp peṟuva tevaṉ	(46)
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvaṉārum, kūṟiṉār eṉka.
aṉpuṭimi, viruntōmpal, iṉiyavi kūṟal, ceynnaṉṟi yaṟital, naṭuvunilimi, aṭakkamuṭimi, oḻukkamuṭimi, piṟaṉil viḻiyāmi, poṟiyuṭimi, aḻukkāṟāmi, veḵkāmi, puṟaṅkūṟāmi, payaṉila collāmi, tīviṉiyaccam, oppuravaṟital, īki eṉpaṉa illaṟa vāḻkkikku vitikkappaṭṭa aṟaṅkaḷātaliṉ, iviyaṉittiyuṅ kikkoḷvār vīṭṭulaki yaṭitaṟku eḷḷaḷavum aiyamiṉṟām.
āyiṉum, makkaḷtoki mikku mānila muḻutum iṭarppaṭum ikkālattil, tuṟavaṟañ ciṟantateṉṟu kūṟit tuṟaviyari ūkkutal vēṇṭum eṉṟē tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
4. tirumaṇamuṅ karaṇamum
ōr āṭavaṉum oru peṇṭum, kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumāka illaṟam naṭatta icintu oṉṟucērvatē maṇamām. maṇattal kalattal allatu kūṭutal. maṇavāḻkkikkeṉṟē iṟivaṉ makkaḷi āṇum peṇṇumāyp paṭittiruppatālum, ataṉiṭattu mikunta aṟappoṟuppuḷḷamiyālum, atu āyiraṅkālattup payir ākiyālum, vāḻkkit tuṇivar iruvarum teyvattiṉmuṉ allatu teyvattiṉpēril palaraṟiya āṇiyiṭṭuk kūṭuvatālum, maṇam teyvattaṉmi peṟṟut tirumaṇam eṉap peṟṟatu. ip peyar, piṉṉar, tirumaṇat toṭarpāṉa viḻāviyuṅ kuṟittatu.
tirumaṇattiṟkuriya oppanta allatu tālikaṭṭuc caṭaṅku karaṇam eṉappaṭum. karaṇam ceyki. atu āṭcipaṟṟic caṭaṅki uṇarttiṟṟu. karaṇattōṭu kūṭiya tirumaṇatti vatuvi maṇam eṉpatu ilakkiya vaḻakku.
mutaṟ kālattil, ellā āṭavarum peṇṭirum, paruvam vantapiṉ vilaṅkum paṟaviyumpōlak karaṇamiṉṟiyē kūṭi vāḻntu vantaṉar. āyiṉ, cila āṭavar, tām maṇanta makaḷiri maṇakkavilliyeṉṟu poyyurittum, avarik kiviṭṭum, avar vāḻvik keṭuttum vantatiṉāl, makkaḷmītu aruḷkoṇṭa tamiḻa muṉivar, karaṇam eṉṉum tirumaṇac caṭaṅki ēṟpaṭuttiṉar. maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷit taṉ niliyāṉa vāḻkkit tuṇiyākak koḷvatāka, palaraṟiyak kaṭavuḷ tirumuṉ cūḷ (āṇi) iṭuvatē karaṇamām.
poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar
aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa	(kaṟpiyal, 4)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam
5. maṇamakkaḷ peyar
kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumāka oruvarukkoruvar vāḻkkit tuṇivarāy illaṟan toṭaṅkum iruvarum, tirumaṇattaṉṟu maṇamakkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. av viruvariyum pirittuk kūṟuṅkāl, maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷ eṉṟum, maṇavāḷaṉ maṇavāṭṭi eṉṟum, peṇ māppiḷḷi (maṇavāḷap piḷḷi) eṉṟum, peṇ piḷḷi eṉṟum, kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. maṇamakaṉip piḷḷi eṉpatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakku.
kaṇavaṉ maṉivi, āṇmakaṉ peṇṭāṭṭi, akamuṭiyāṉ aka muṭiyāḷ, illāḷaṉ illāḷ eṉṉum peyariṇikaḷ tirumaṇattiṟkup piṉpu illaṟakkālattil niliyāka vaḻaṅkuvaṉa. maṇāṭṭuppeṇ eṉpatu muṟiyē māṭṭuppeṇ nāṭṭuppeṇ eṉa maruvi, marumakaḷ eṉṉum poruḷil, tañci vaṭṭārattil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. talivaṉ talivi, talimakaṉ talimakaḷ, kiḻavaṉ kiḻatti (nāṭukiḻavōr) eṉpaṉa, aracak kulattārkkuriyaṉavāy, illaṟam neṭukalum vaḻaṅkum ilakkiya vaḻakkām. ivaṟṟuḷ, kiḻavaṉ kiḻatti eṉpaṉa maṉikkiḻavaṉ maṉikkiḻatti eṉṉum vaḻakkil potumakkaṭkum vaḻaṅkum. maṉikkiḻattiyi vāḻvaraci eṉpatu nellināṭṭu nalvaḻakku.
tiruvaḷḷuvar, kaṇavaṉukkut talimi tōṉṟa maṉiviyi vāḻkkit tuṇiyeṉṟu kūṟiyiruppiṉum, illaṟatti irupakaṭ ṭorucakaṭu eṉṟum, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyar kātaloṟṟumiyi ōrāviṉuk kirukōṭu tōṉṟiṉāṟpōl eṉṟum, uvamittuk kūṟum vaḻakkiṟkum. ikkālat tōṅkivarum camanilik karuttiṟkum ēṟpa, iruvariyum taṉittaṉi vāḻkkit tuṇiyeṉṟō, vāḻkkit tuṇivaṉ vāḻkkit tuṇivi eṉṟō, kuṟippatu kuṟṟamākātu.
6. aṉpum kātalum kāmamum
aṉpu kātal kāmam eṉpaṉa, parunōkkil oṉṟāyiṉum, nuṇ ṇōkkil vēṟupaṭṭaṉa.
aṉpeṉpatu, ēcuvum puttarum pōl ellāriṭattum kāṭṭum nēyam. atu aṟamākavum aṟaviṉikaṭkellām kāraṇamākavum karutappaṭum.
aṉpillā rellān tamakkuriyar aṉpuṭiyār 
eṉpum uriyar piṟarkku	(72)
aṉpum aṟaṉum uṭittāyiṉ ilvāḻkki
paṇpum payaṉum atu	(45)
eṉṉum kuṟaḷkaḷ iti yuṇarttum.
kātal eṉpatu, oruvari yoruvar iṉṟiyamiyāk kaḻiperu nēyamāy iruvariṭi nikaḻvatu. atu, kōpperuñ cōḻaṉum picirāntiyārum pōlum naṇpariṭattum, peṟṟōrum piḷḷiyum pōlum uṟaviṉariṭattum, pūtappāṇṭiyaṉum avaṉ tēviyum pōlum kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyariṭattum, amivatu. atu ariya piṟavik kuṇam.
kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum iṟantami kēṭṭa tāymār uṭaluyir tuṟantami kāṇka. kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyariṭippaṭṭa kātal uṇmiyil iruvarkkum potuvēṉum, atu ciṟappāka maṉivikkē allatu maṉivi yarkkē uriyatu eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa koḷki, toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
kāmam eṉpatu, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyariṭattēyē allatu āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupāliṭiyēyē, nikaḻakkūṭiya ciṟappuvaki nēyam. kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyar illaṟa viṉpam tuyttaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa nēyam eṉṉum poruḷilēyē kāmam eṉṉuñ colli āṇṭu, iṉpattuppālik kāmattuppāl eṉak kuṟittaṉar vaḷḷuvar. ac col, iṉṟu kalavi vēṭki eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa poruḷil vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, aruḷ eṉpatu yātōveṉiṉ, atu uyarntōr tāḻntōriṭattuk kāṭṭum irakkam eṉka.

1. paṇṭit tamiḻmaṇam
paṇṭit tamiḻ maṇam eṉṟu iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭavi, tamiḻakattil, toṉṟutoṭṭup pirāmaṇar peruntokiyāy vantu palavūrkaḷilum taṅkumaḷavum, tamiḻap pārppārālum kulattalivarālum tamiḻil naṭattappeṟṟu vanta maṇaṅkaḷākum.
pirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭu pukattoṭaṅkiyatu ēṟattāḻak ki.mu. 2000 eṉiṉum, avar mutalaṭiyilēyē peruntokiyiṉarāy iṅku vantavarallar. mutaṉmutal iṅkuk kāl vitta pirāmaṇar viralviṭṭu eṇṇattakkavarē yāvar. kiṟittava vūḻi toṭaṅkumvari iṭiyiṭṭiṭiyiṭṭuc ciṟuciṟu kūṭṭattārākavē avar vantu koṇṭiruntaṉar. ki.pi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉ, pallava aracar kālattiltāṉ, avar peruvāriyāka vaṭanāṭṭiliruntu kuṭiyēṟṟappaṭṭaṉar. itip piṟkālac cēracōḻa pāṇṭiyarum piṉpaṟṟiṉar.
māmutu pārppāṉ maṟivaḻi kāṭṭiṭa eṉṟu cilappatikāram pirāmaṇa allatu āriyap pārppaṉap purōkitattik kuṟittiruppatu, kaṭiccaṅkak kālattil nakaraṅkaḷilum mānakaraṅkaḷilum perumpālum aracariṭiyum vaṇikariṭiyum irunta nilimiyittāṉ kuṟikkum. iṉṟum pirāmaṇarillāta pala nāṭṭuppuṟattūrkaḷ iruppatālum, cila tamiḻakkulattār pirāmaṇap purōkitamiṉṟit tam maṇaṅkaḷi naṭattikkoḷvatālum, pirāmaṇar vanta piṉpum, kaṭiccaṅkak kālamvari perumpāl tamiḻar maṇaṅkaḷ tamiḻ marapilēyē naṭantuvantaṉa eṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum.
tamiḻappārppār, paṇṭāram, pulavaṉ, kurukkaḷ, tiru(kkaḷ), pūcāri, uvaccaṉ, ōtuvāṉ, pōṟṟi, nampi, arumikkāraṉ (puṭavikkāraṉ), vaḷḷuvaṉ mutaliya palvēṟu vakuppār āvar. pārppāṉ kōyiṟ karumaṅkaḷip pārppavaṉ.
antaṇaṉ, aiyaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ mutaṉmutal tamiḻat tuṟavi yariyē kuṟittatupōṉṟu, pārppāṉ eṉṉum peyarum mutaṉmutal tamiḻap pūcāriyiyē kuṟittatu. turi eṉṉum tamiḻ allatu teluṅkac col nam nāṭṭil taṅkum mēṉāṭṭārkku variyaṟukkappaṭṭatupōṉṟē, antaṇar, pārppār eṉṉum peyarkaḷum pirāmaṇarkku variyaṟukkappaṭṭaṉa eṉka. iṉṉum itaṉ viriviyellām eṉatu tamiḻarkula marapu eṉṉum nūliṟ kaṇṭu koḷka. tamiḻaruḷ, pārppār eṉpār illaṟattārum, antaṇar eṉpār tuṟavaṟattārum āvar.
kulattalivarāvār nāṭṭāṇmikkāraṉ, nāṭṭāṉ, periya taṉakkāraṉ, ampalakkāraṉ, ūrkkavuṇṭaṉ, ūrkkuṭumpaṉ, paṭṭakkāraṉ, ūrāḷi, mūppaṉ eṉa āṅkāṅku vevvēṟu peyarāl aḻikkappeṟum kulaviyal ūrāṭciyāḷar.
paṇṭit tamiḻmaṇa muṟiyilēyē iṉṉum pala maṇaṅkaḷ naṭantu varuvatāl, avaṟṟiyun taḻuvumāṟu paṇṭit tamiḻ maṇam  eṉṉum talippiṟkup paṇṭik kālattil ellāt tamiḻarum kiyāṇṭa maṇamuṟi eṉṟu poruḷ koḷka.
1. maṇavaki
(1) ulakiyaṟ pākupāṭu
paṇṭit tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷiṉ ulakiyaṟ pākupāṭu, piṉ varumāṟu palvēṟu muṟi paṟṟiyatākum.
1. koḷmuṟi paṟṟiyatu
paṇṭit tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ, peṇkōṭal muṟipaṟṟi, (1)koṭimaṇam, (2) kātal maṇam, (3) kavarvu (vaṉkōḷ)  maṇam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum.
(1) koṭimaṇam
koṭimaṇamāvatu, maṇamakaṉēṉum avaṉ peṟṟōrēṉum maṇamakaḷ peṟṟōri aṭuttuk kēṭka, avar koṭuppatu. atu, (1) tāṉak koṭi, (2) vilikkoṭi, (3) nilippāṭṭukkoṭi eṉa mūvakittu.
tāṉakkoṭiyāvatu, oruvar tam makaḷit takka ēḻi maṇāḷaṉukkut tāmē ellāc celavum ēṟṟu maṇañceytu vippatu, vilikkoṭiyāvatu, oruvaṉ taṉ makaḷukku īṭāka oru tokiyi oru celvaṉiṭam peṟṟukkoṇṭu, avaḷi avaṉukku maṉiviyākak koṭuppatu; vili viṟpaṉi; itu kīḻōriṭam aruki nikaḻvatu; nilippāṭṭuk koṭiyāvatu, oruvar tam makaḷi oruvaṉukku ētēṉum nilippāṭu allatu akkuttup (condition) paṟṟi maṉiviyākak koṭuppatu. an nilippāṭu, ittuṇip parican taralvēṇṭumeṉṟum, ittuṇik kālam peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōrkku uḻittalvēṇṭu meṉṟum, iṉṉa maṟacceyalip purital vēṇṭu
meṉṟum, maṇamakaḷi iṉṉa kaliyil vellutal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, maṇattiṉpiṉ peṇ vīṭṭilēyē vatital vēṇṭumeṉṟum, palvēṟu tiṟappaṭṭatā yirukkum.
maṟacceyalkaḷ, kollēṟu kōṭal (ēṟu taḻuval), tiripaṉṟi yeytal, pulippāl kaṟattal, koṭuvilaṅku kōṟal, pakivariyaḻittal, vilnāṇēṟ ṟal, peruṅkal tūkkal mutaliyaṉa. ēṟu - kāḷi, kōṭal - aṭakkal, 
kōṟal - kollutal. tiripaṉṟi yeytalāvatu, mikavuyarattil virivākac cuḻaṉṟu koṇṭirukkum ciṟiya paṉṟiyuruvi orē taṭaviyil eytu vīḻttutal.
paṇṭik kālattil mullinilattilirunta āyariṭi, ēṟu taḻuvi maṇappatē kulamarapāka iruntuvantatu. ōr āyarpāṭiyil  allatu cēriyil, oru peṇkuḻanti piṟantavuṭaṉ ataṉ peyarukku oru cēṅkaṉṟip peṟṟōr otukki vippar. ak kaṉṟik kāyaṭiyāmalum, vēliyil vayakkāmalum, koḻutta ūṭṭaṅ koṭuttuk kompu cīvik kūrākkuvar. āṇṭutōṟum, kuṟitta naṉṉāḷil, maṇapparuvamaṭinta maṅkiyarkkuriya kāḷikaḷiyellām oru toḻuvattil aṭittu vittu, ovvoṉṟākat tiṟantuviṭuvar. makkaḷ āravārattiyum ēṟukōṭpaṟi muḻakkattiyum, kaṇṭum kēṭṭum, maruṇṭōṭum ovvoru kollēṟṟiyum, māṇiyarāṉa āya iḷiñar piṭittu niṟutta muyalvar. palar kollēṟukaḷāṟ kuttik kollappaṭuvatu muṇṭu. oru kollēṟṟi evaṉ piṭittaṭakki niṟuttu kiṉṟāṉō avaṉ av ēṟṟiṟkuriya āyamakaḷi maṇappāṉ. im maṇamuṟi, kalittoki eṉṉum caṅka nūlil, mullikkaliyil, virivākak kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. vaṭanāṭṭiṟ kaṇṇaṉ ēḻ ēṟutaḻuvi nappiṉṉiyi maṇantatu it tamiḻ marapē.
iṅṅaṉam oru maṟacceyali maṇamakaṉatu takutiyākak koḷḷum vaḻakkattiṉāl, oru kulattāriṉ maṟattiṟam mēṉmēlum vaḷarntu varuvatuṭaṉ, avarkkup piṟakkum kuḻantikaḷum iyalpāka maṟavuṇarcci viñciyavāyiruk
kiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu, maṟañciṟanta āṭavariyē peṇṭir maṇakkum ēṟpāṭṭi, pāliyal terippu (sexual selection) eṉpar ṭārviṉ pēraṟiñar. oru kulattār tamakkuḷḷēyē neṭukalum maṇantu varuvatāl ēṟpaṭum eccaviyaṟ kuṟi
pāṭṭiṟku īṭu ceyvatu, ēṟutaḻuval pōṉṟa maṇamakaṉ maṟaviyal takuti ēṟpāṭē.
ēṟu taḻuval eṉṉum paṇṭi vaḻakkamē, iṉṟu kaḷḷar maṟavariṭic callikkaṭṭu eṉṟum, mañcuviraṭṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. āyar iv vaḻakkatti nīṇṭa kālamāka aṭiyōṭu viṭṭuviṭṭatiṉāl, tam muṉṉōriṉ maṟatti muṟṟum iḻantuviṭṭaṉar. callikkaṭṭu māṭṭut toḻuvi iṉṟum pāṭi allatu pāṭivācal eṉṟaḻippatu, iv vaḻakkam paṇṭik kālattil āyarpāṭiyil nikaḻntatu eṉpati uṇarttum.
tirumaṇattaṉṟu maṇamakkaḷ ūrvalam varuvatum, cila ciṟṟūrkaḷil ayalūr maṇamakaṉ vantu oru peṇṇi maṇantu mīḷumpōtu, avvūr iḷiñar iḷavaṭṭak kācu eṉṉum kinnīṭṭam kēṭpatum, paṇṭik kālattil oru peṇṇi nōkkip pala iḷiñar pōṭṭiyiṭṭup porutatik kuṟippāy uṇarttum.
āṇmakap pēṟillāta peṟṟōr, tam marumakaṉit tam illat tilēyē iruttikkoḷvar. iv ēṟpāṭṭi illattam eṉpar kaṉṉaṭar.
(2) kātal maṇam
kātal maṇamāvatu, ōr āṭavaṉum oru peṇṭum peṟṟōrik kēḷātum piṟarukkut teriyātum oruvariyoruvar kātalittu, tāmē kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumākak kūṭikkoḷvatu. itu, kaḷavil toṭaṅkuvatum kaṟpil toṭaṅkuvatum eṉa iruvakittu. kaḷaveṉpatu maṟivu; kaṟpeṉpatu veḷippaṭi nī iṉṉavā ṟoḻuka vēṇṭum eṉa maṇamakaḷ tirumaṇa āciriyaṉāl kaṟpikkappaṭum nilimi kaṟpu eṉpar.
kaḷavil toṭaṅkuvatu, irumāta ellikkuḷ eṉṟēṉum veḷip paṭṭuk kaṟpāka māṟiviṭum. ik kaḷavu veḷippāṭu, (1) uṭaṉpōkku, (2) aṟattoṭu nili eṉṉum iruvakikaḷuḷ oṉṟāl ēṟpaṭum, uṭaṉpōk kāvatu, kaḷavoḻukkam taṭippaṭṭaviṭattu allatu kātaliyiṉ peṟṟōr avaḷit tara iciyāviṭattu, kātalaṉ avaḷi vēṟṟūrkkēṉum taṉ vīṭṭiṟkēṉum aḻittukkoṇṭu pōyviṭal. aṟattoṭu niliyāvatu, kātaliyiṉ melivu kaṇṭu atip pōkkuvataṟku, avaḷ peṟṟōr kaṭṭuvicci (kuṟikāri), vēlaṉ (mantirakkāraṉ) mutaliyōriṉ tuṇi vēṇṭum pōtō, ataṟku muṉṉatākavō, kātali tāṉākavēṉum taṉ tōḻi vāyilākavēṉum taṉ kātalaṉippaṟṟit terivittal.
kātalar oru nāḷuṅ kaḷavoḻukkamiṉṟik kaṟpākavē tam kūṭṭu vāḻkkiyit toṭaṅkuvatumuṇṭu. iv iruvakit toṭakkamum, karaṇattōṭu kūṭiyatākavum irukkalām; kūṭātatākavu mirukkalām. ak karaṇamum, maṇamakaṉ tāṉē ceyvippatākavu mirukkalām; maṇamakaḷ peṟṟōrik koṇṭu ceyvippatākavu mirukkalām.
iṅkuk kaṟpeṉpatu uṇmiyil kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ veḷippāṭē yāyiṉum, atu potuvākak karaṇattoṭu kūṭiyatākavē koḷḷappaṭum.
kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu karaṇamoṭu puṇarak
koḷaṟkuri marapiṉ kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyik
koṭikkuri marapiṉōr koṭuppakkoḷ vatuvē   (kaṟpiyal,1)
koṭuppōr iṉṟiyum karaṇam uṇṭē
puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya kāli yāṉa	(mēṟpaṭi 2)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiyam.
uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟa kātalaṉ, taṉṉūr vēṟṟūrāyiṉ pōṉaviṭattum kātali yūrēyāyiṉum, perumpālum taṉ maṉiyilēyē ati vittuk koḷvaṉ. uṟṟārk kuriyar poṟṟoṭi makaḷir eṉpatālum.
uyiriṉum ciṟantaṉṟu nāṇē nāṇiṉum 
ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpucciṟan taṉṟu	(tol. kaḷavu. 22)
eṉpatālum, kātaliyiṉ peṟṟōr kātalaṉi oppukkoṇṭu, avaṉ vatuvi maṇattit tam maṉiyil naṭatta virumpiṉ, av viruppam niṟivēṟuvatu
muṇṭu. yār maṉiyil vatuvi nikaḻiṉum, vatuvikku muṉ maṇamakaḷ kālil avaḷ peṟṟōrāl aṇiyappaṭṭirunta cilampi nīkkutaṟku oru caṭaṅku ceyyappaṭum. atu cilampukaḻi nōṉpu eṉappaṭum.
iti,
nummaṉic cilampu kaḻīi ayariṉum
emmaṉi vatuvi naṉmaṇaṅ kaḻikeṉac
colliṉ evaṉō maṟṟē veṉvēl
miyaṟa viḷaṅkiya kaḻalaṭip
poyval kāḷiyi yīṉṟa tāykkē	(399) 
eṉṉum aiṅkuṟunūṟṟuc ceyyuḷāl aṟiyalām.
itu, uṭaṉkoṇṭupōṉa kātalaṉ mīṇṭu vantu, taṉ kātaliyit taṉ illattiṟkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟa viṭattu, avaṉ tāy avaḷukkuc cilampukaḻi nōṉpu ceykiṉṟāḷeṉak kēṭṭa naṟṟāy (peṟṟa tāy), aṅku niṉṟum vantārkkuc colliyatu.
(num - um. cilampu - taṇṭi. kaḻīi - kaḻittu. ayariṉum - koṇṭāṭi ṉālum. kaḻikeṉa - naṭakkaveṉṟu. evaṉō - eṉṉa. veṉ - veṟṟi. miyaṟa - kuṟṟam nīṅka, kaḻal - vīrakkālaṇi, kāḷi - vīraṉākiya kātalaṉ).
karaṇamiṉṟiyum kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumāka iru kātalar icintu vāḻak kūṭumāyiṉum, karaṇattoṭu toṭaṅkum illaṟamē ellārālum pōṟṟap paṭuvatām; aḵtillākkāl, atu vippu eṉa iḻintōrālum tūṟṟap paṭuvatē.
kātalar vāḻvu kaḷavoḻukkattōṭu toṭaṅkiṉ meyyuṟu puṇarcciyum, allākkāl uḷḷappuṇarccimāttiriyum, kaṟpiṟkumuṉ peṟupavarāvar.
peṟṟōrum piṟarum muṭittu vikkum tirumaṇattilum maṇamakkaḷ iruvarkkum kātaluṇṭākalā meṉiṉum, kātal maṇameṉṟu ciṟappittuc collappeṟuvatu ōr āṭavaṉum oru peṇṭum tāmāka vāḻkki oppantañ ceytukoḷvatē.
maṭalēṟṟam
mutupaḻaṅkālattil, oru kaṭuṅkātalaṉ allatu kātaṟpittaṉ avaṉ kātaliyi maṇattaṟku avaḷ peṟṟōr iciyāviṭiṉ, avaḷip peṟutaṟku maṭalēṟṟam eṉṉum uyirccētattiṟ kiṭamāṉa oru vaṉmuṟiyik kiyāḷva tuṇṭu. atu ikkālattuc cattiyākkirakameṉṉum pāṭukiṭappup pōṉṟatu.
maṭalēṟat tuṇinta kātalaṉ, nīrccīli oṉṟēyuṭuttu uṭampelāñ cāmpaṟpūci erukkamāliyaṇintu, taṉ kātaliyiṉ ūr naṭuvē tavaniliyi lamarntu, avaḷ uruvi varinta oru tuṇiyik kiyilēnti, ati uṟṟunōkkiya vaṇṇamāy vāḷāviruppaṉ. ataṉik kaṇṭa av vūrār nī āyvu (cōtaṉi) tarukiṉṟāyā? eṉak kēṭpar. avaṉ tarukiṉṟēṉ eṉiṉ, paṉaṅkarukku maṭṭiyāṟ ceyta oru poyk kutiriyiṉmēl avaṉiyēṟṟit teru neṭuka iḻuttuc celvar. aṅṅaṉam iḻukkumpōtu, avaṉ uṭampiṟ karukkaṟuttuk kāyam paṭṭaviṭamellām veḷuttut tōṉṟiṉ, avaṉukku avaṉ kātaliyi maṇamuṭittu vippar; allākkāl viyār. itu kātalar tāmākak kūṭuvataṉṟāyiṉum, orupuṭi yoppumipaṟṟi iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭatu.
kātaṟ pāṭṭukaḷ
iv vulakiṟ ciṟantatu illaṟa iṉpam. atilum, kātalar nukarvatu kari yaṟṟatu. avar ōrūrarāyum oruvariyoruvar muṉṉaṟintavarāyum irukka vēṇṭumeṉṉum yāppuṟavilli. karuṅkaṭaluppiṟkum, karumali nārattikkum tontam ēṟpaṭuvatupōl, neṭṭiṭippaṭṭa iruvar ōriṭattu oruvariyoruvar kaṇṭu kātalikkavum nērum. attaki nilimi vāynta oru kātalaṉ kūṟṟāka vuḷḷa pāṭṭoṉṟu varumāṟu :
yāyum ñāyum yārā kiyarō
entiyum nuntiyum emmuṟik kēḷir
yāṉum nīyum evvaḻi aṟitum
cempulap peyaṉīr pōla
aṉpuṭi neñcam tāṅkalan taṉavē.	(kuṟun. 40)
(yāy - eṉ tāy, ñāy - uṉ tāy. enti - eṉ tanti. nunti - uṉ tanti. kēḷir - uṟaviṉar. cempulappeyalnīr pōla - civanta nilattiṟ cērnta nīrum civappatu pōla.)
iṉi, oru kātali kūṟṟāka vuḷḷa pāṭṭoṉṟu varumāṟu :
irāmaḻi peyta īral īrattuḷ
paṉinukaṅ koṇṭu yāṉiyēr pūṭṭi
veḷḷi virittup poṉṉē viḷiyiṉum
vēṇṭēṉ piṟantakat tīṇṭiya vāḻvē
ceṅkēḻ varaku pacuṅkatir koytu
kaṉṟu kāttuk kuṉṟil uṇakki
ūṭupatar pōkkimuṉ utaviṉōrkku kutavik
kāṭukaḻi intaṉam pāṭupārt teṭuttuk
kuppik kīri uppiṉṟi ventati
iraval tālam parivuṭaṉ vāṅkic
cōṟatu koṇṭu pīṟal aṭittē
oṉṟuviṭ ṭorunāḷ tiṉṟu kiṭappiṉum
naṉṟē tōḻinam kaṇavaṉ vāḻvē.
(nukam-nukattaṭi. īṇṭiya-celvamikunta. uṇakki-kāyavittu. intaṉam-viṟaku. pāṭu-viḻutal. tālam-uṇkalam. parivu-varuttam.pīṟal-cēlikkiḻivu).
(3) kavarvu maṇam
kavarvu maṇamāvatu, oru peṇṇip peṟṟōricivum avaḷ icivumiṉṟi, valintu paṟṟutal.
atu, ūrukku veḷiyē taṉittu niṟkum ōr iḷampeṇṇi, vēṟṟūrāṉ oruvaṉ valintu paṟṟikkoṇṭu pōy, avaḷi maṉiviyākak koḷvatu pōṉṟatu.
oru maṟakkuṭip peṇṇi maṇakka virumpiya vēntaṉ, ak kuṭiyār ataṟkiciyāviṭattu, perum paṭiyoṭu ceṉṟu avarōṭu pōr purivatuṇṭu. am maṟavar tam kuṭiyiṉ māṉattik kāttaṟku añcātu etirppar. itu makaṭpāṟ kāñci eṉappaṭum.
nikarttumēl vanta vēntaṉoṭu mutukuṭi
makaṭpā ṭañciya makaṭpā vāṉum	(puṟattiṇiyiyal, 24) 
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyatu ituvē. itaṉiyē, maṟam eṉṉuṅ kalampaka vuṟuppu, aracaṉ peṇkēṭṭu viṭutta tirumukattik koṇarnta tūtaṉi nōkki, maṟavar eccarittum accuṟuttiyum viṭuppatākak kūṟum.
oru maṇappeṇṇiṉ aḻakip paḻikkumpōtu, avaḷ eṉṉa oru periya ciṟiyā? eṉṟu peṇṭir kūṟum vaḻakkam, oru kālattiṟ pēraḻakuṭiya peṇkaḷ valiyōrālum aracālum ciṟipiṭikkappaṭṭamiyi uṇarttum.
ōr aracaṉ, taṉ pakiyaracaṉi veṉṟu allatu koṉṟu, avaṉuṭiya tēviyaric ciṟipiṭittu vantu vēḷam eṉṉum ciṟiccāliyil iṭṭu avamāṉappaṭuttuvatu, kavarvu maṇattiṉ pāṟpaṭātu.
2. kulamuṟi paṟṟiyatu
paṇṭimuṟit tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ, kulamuṟipaṟṟi akamaṇam (Endogamy), puṟamaṇam (Exogamy) eṉa iruvakippaṭum.
akamaṇamāvatu, oru kulattār tam kulattiṟkuḷḷēyē maṇattal; puṟamaṇamāvatu oru kulap pirivār tam piriviṟkuḷ maṇavātu vēṟoru pirivil maṇattal.
iṉṟuḷḷa kulaṅkaḷuḷ, kalappuk kulaṅkaḷ tavira ēṉiya vellām akamaṇattaṉavē. tiṇimayakkam ēṟpaṭu muṉ kuṟavar, āyar, vēṭṭuvar, uḻavar, nuḷiyar (cempaṭavar) eṉat tamiḻar aintiṇi makkaḷāy vevvēṟu nilattil vāḻntapōtu, avarkkuḷ perumpāl vaḻakkamāy nikaḻntatu akamaṇamē.
kulappirivukaḷ nāṭu, ūr, kulam, kūṭṭam, kiḷi, vakuppu, illam, kari mutaliyaṉavākap palvēṟu tiṟappaṭum.
nāṭum ūrum illum kuṭiyum
piṟappum ciṟappum	(1060)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
cēlam māvaṭṭattuc cērvarāyaṉmali, paccimali, kollimali mutaliya malikaḷil vāḻum tamiḻ maliyāḷik kulattil, (1) periya maliyāḷi, (2) pacci maliyāḷi, (3) kolli maliyāḷi eṉṉum mūṉṟum uṭkulaṅkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ, kolli maliyāḷi eṉṉum uṭkulattil (1) munnāṭṭu maliyāḷi, (2) nālnāṭṭumaliyāḷi, (3) añcūr maliyāḷi eṉṉum nāṭṭut tokutiyil, (1) mayilam, (2) tiruppuli, (3) iṭappuli, (4) piṟakari, (5) ciṟṟūr eṉṉum aintum nāṭukaḷ eṉṉum uṭpirivukaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ, ciṟṟūr eṉṉum nāṭṭil, (1) pīlaṉ, (2) mūkkāṇṭi, (3) pūcaṉ, (4) māṇikkaṉ, (5) tiruvicci, (6) kaṇṇaṉ, (7) tillāṉ eṉṉum ēḻum vakuppukaḷ eṉṉum uṭpirivukaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉ aintum oru tokutit tāyāti vakuppukaḷ; piṉ iraṇṭum maṟṟoru tokutit tāyāti vakuppukaḷ. iv iru tokutikaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟum aṭutta tokutiyuḷaṉṟit taṉ tokutiyuḷ maṇappatilli. itu puṟamaṇamām.
koṅkuvēḷāḷar kulattiṉ uṭpirivukaḷāṉa, tūraṅkulam cempōttaṅ kulam, āntikkulam mutaliya irupattiraṇṭu kulaṅkaḷum, puṟamaṇattaṉavām. ik kulaṅkaḷ kūṭṭameṉavumpaṭum.
orē kulattuḷ toṭarntu maṇaṅkaḷ naṭipeṟuvatāl viḷiyum kēṭṭi vilakkutaṟkē, kulappirivukaṭkup puṟamaṇam vitikkappaṭṭatākat teri kiṉṟatu.
aracar piṟakulattil tām peṇkoṇṭālum, tam makaḷirip piṟakulattārkkuk koṭuppatilli. itu uyarmaṇattiṉ (Hypergamy) pāṟpaṭum.
3. maṇamakkaḷ tokipaṟṟiyatu
maṇamakkaḷ tokipaṟṟi, tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ (1) oru maṉiyam (Monogamy), (2) palmaṉiyam (Polygamy), (3) palkaṇavam (Polyandry) eṉa mūvakippaṭum.
oru kālattil orē maṉiviyuṭimi orumaṉiyam; orē kālattil pala maṉiviyaruṭimi palmaṉiyam; orē kālattil pala kaṇavaruṭimi palkaṇavam.
paṇṭik kālattil, pala aracarum talivarum, palmaṉiyattit taḻuviyatuṭaṉ potumakaḷirākiya parattiyaruṭaṉum toṭarpu koṇ ṭiruntaṉar. parattiyaruḷ, vēṟāka ōr illattil iruppavaḷ iṟparatti yeṉṟum, parattiyar cēriyil iruppavaḷ cēripparattiyeṉṟum, cēripparattiyaruḷ ciṟantu kātalikkappaṭupavaḷ kātaṟparattiyeṉṟum collappaṭuvar.
parattiyar pōṉṟē potumakaḷirāyuḷḷa iṉṉoru vakuppār kaṇikiyar (kūttiyar) eṉpār. kālaṅkaṇittāṭupavar kaṇikiyar. avar nāṭakak kaṇikiyar kōyiṟ kaṇikiyar eṉa irucārār.
iṉi, potumakaḷirallātu, karaṇamiṉṟi maṉiviyarpōl vittuk koḷḷappaṭum vippu eṉṉum makaḷirumuṇṭu. avar kaṉṉiyarākavō kaṭṭuppaṭṭavarākavō kimpeṇṭirākavō irukkalām.
paṇṭittamiḻar potuvākap paṇpāṭṭilum paltuṟippaṭṭa nākarikattilum ciṟantiruntārēṉum, peṇṇiṉpattuṟiyil perumpālum nekiḻnta neṟiyuṭiyarākavē yiruntaṉar. aracar kaṭṭumaṭṭiṉṟic ciṟṟiṉpatti nukarntu vantatiṉālēyē, ulā, maṭal, kātal mutaliya akapporuṭ paṉuvalkaḷum (pirapantaṅkaḷ) parattiyiṟ pirivu eṉṉum kōvik kiḷavikkottum iṭikkālat teḻuntaṉa. mēṉāṭṭār in nāvalan tēyattiṟku vanta piṉpē, in neṟitiṟampiya nili tiruntat toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
palkaṇavam peṇ pañcattālum peṇkoliyālum ēṟpaṭuvatu. atu iṉṟu nīlamalit totuvariṭiyē uḷḷatu.
4. maṇamakaḷnili paṟṟiyatu
maṇamakaḷ nilipaṟṟi, tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ. (1) kaṉṉi maṇam, (2) kaṭṭuppaṭṭavaḷ maṇam, (3) kimpeṇ maṇam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum.
kaṉṉi maṇam, oru kaṉṉip peṇṇip putitāka maṇattal; kaṭṭup paṭṭavaḷ maṇam, oru muṟi maṇakkappaṭṭut tīrappaṭṭavaḷi maṇattal; kimpeṇ maṇam, kaṇavaṉi yiḻantavaḷi maṇattal. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉavi yiraṇṭum iḻintōr maṇamākavum iṭittarattōr maṇamākavum iruntuvantirukkiṉṟaṉa.
oru muṟi maṇakkappaṭṭavaḷi maṟumaṇañ ceytal ciṟappillāta tākak karutappaṭuvatāl, kaṭṭuppaṭṭavaḷ maṇattil maṇamuḻā (kaliyāṇa mēḷam) iṉṟiyē tāli kaṭṭappaṭuvatumuṇṭu. atu kaṭṭuttāliyeṉṟum, maṇamuḻavu uḷḷatu koṭṭuttirumaṇam eṉṟum peyar peṟum. kimpeṇ maṇam perumpālum kaṭṭuttāliyākavē yirukkum.
(2) ilakkaṇap pākupāṭu
iṉi, tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ, maṇamakkaḷiṉ kātalnilipaṟṟit tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷi, (1) kikkiḷi, (2) aṉpiṉintiṇi, (3) peruntiṇi eṉa mūvakiyāka vakuttuk kūṟum.
kikkiḷiyāvatu, āṭavaṉ peṇṭu ākiya iruvaruḷḷum oruvarukkē kātal iruppatu; aṉpiṉ aintiṇiyāvatu, av iruvarukkum kātal iruppatu; peruntiṇiyāvatu, av iruvarukkum kātal illā tiruppatu allatu oruvariyoruvar valintukoḷvatu. im mūṉṟum, muṟiyē orutalik kāmam, irutalik kāmam, poruntāk kāmam eṉṟum collappeṟum. ki eṉpatu pakkam. kiḷi eṉpatu nēyam. ākavē kikkiḷi eṉpatu orutalik kātal. aintiṇi eṉpaṉa, kuṟiñci, mulli, pāli, marutam, neytal eṉṉum ainnilaṅkaḷ. kuṟiñci maliyum malicārnta iṭamum; mulli maliyaṟṟa ceṭiyuṅkāṭum pulveḷiyum; pāli nīrum niḻalumiṉṟi vaṟṟi vaṟaṇṭa iṭam; marutam nīrvaḷamum nilavaḷamum oruṅkē yuḷḷa vayalnāṭu; neytal kaṭalum kaṭaṟkariyum. iv ainnilaṅkaṭkum potuvākak koḷḷappaṭṭatiṉāl, irutalik kātal aṉpiṉ aintiṇi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
peruntiṇi perumpakuti makkaḷ maṇamuṟikaḷum perumpālaṉa vaṟṟit  taṉṉuḷ aṭakki niṟpatāl, poruntāk kāmam peruntiṇi yeṉap paṭṭatu. kavarvu maṇaṅkaḷum, iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa ellāp puṇarcci vakikaḷum, peruntiṇiyē. kātaloṭu poruntāmiyum neṟiyoṭu poruntāmiyumpaṟṟi, peruntiṇi poruntāk kāmam eṉappaṭṭatu.
kikkiḷikkum peruntiṇikkum nilamillātapaṭi, aivaki nilaṅkaḷiyum naṭunāyakamāṉa aṉpiṉ aintiṇikkē urimiyākki viṭṭataṉāl, makkaṭku nalam payavāta ēṉi yirumaṇa muṟikaḷum in nilavulakattillātu nīṅkal vēṇṭum eṉpatu, muṉṉit tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlāriṉ karuttākum.
maṇamakkaḷiṉ icivik kēḷātu peṟṟōrē muṭittu vikkum tirumaṇaṅkaḷil, maṇamakaṉukku allatu maṇamakaḷukku maṭṭum kātal iruppiṉ, atu kikkiḷi maṇamām; iruvarukkum iruppiṉ, atu aṉpiṉ aintiṇiyām; oruvarukkum illāviṭiṉ, peruntiṇiyiṉ pāṟpaṭuvatām.
maṇamakkaḷ iruvaruḷ oruvarukku maṭṭum kātal irukkumpōtu, ēṉiyavarum etirppiṉṟi icintiruntāltāṉ, atu kikkiḷi maṇamākum. allākkāl atu peruntiṇiyē. orutalik kāmam eṉpatu kātaliṉṟiyum icintuvarum peṇṇi āṭavaṉum āṭavaṉip peṇṇum maṇappatē yaṉṟi, iciyāp peṇṇiyum āṭavaṉiyum valitil maṇappataṉṟu.
orutalik kāmam eṉṉum muṟiyil kikkiḷiyum, peruntiṇiyum okkumēṉum, muṉṉatu neṟippaṭṭateṉṟum, piṉṉatu neṟitiṟampiyateṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum.
kāmavuṇarcciyillāc ciṟumiyiṭattum, kāmavuṇarcciyiruntum taṉmēṟ kātal koḷḷāp paruvappeṇṇiṭattum, oruvaṉ kātal moḻikaḷik kūṟi iṉpuṟuvatu kikkiḷik kuṟippēyaṉṟik kikkiḷi maṇamākātu.
irutaliyuṅ kātaluḷḷaviṭattum, mutiyāḷoṭu kūṭutalum, kūṭṭa iṭiyīṭupāṭṭāl ēṟpaṭum kāmap pittamum kaḻineñcaḻivum, peruntiṇi yeṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl; viṇṇiṉpatti yottatoru peṇ ṇiṉpatti maṇṇulakōrkku vakuttaṉar muṉṉit tamiḻ ilakkaṇiyar eṉka.
ittakiya iṉpam mikamika aritāy orucilarkkē kiṭṭuvatāyiṉum, ati ōr aḷaviyākakkoṇṭu kūṟiyatu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam eṉappaṭum.
2. maṇattoki
paṇṭit tamiḻ maṇaṅkaṭku ittuṇi eṉṉun toki varampilli. maṇapparuvam maṇamakaṉukkup patiṉāṟāṇṭeṉṟum, maṇamakaḷukkup paṉṉīrāṇṭeṉṟum, nūlkaḷ kūṟum. paṇṭik kālat tamiḻmakkaḷ ettuṇi valimi mikkavarāy iruntiruppiṉum, nūlkaḷil maṇamakkaṭkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa paruvam muntiyatē. akkālattil tirumaṇac caṭṭa varampiṉmiyālum, iṉpac ciṟappoṉṟiyē makkaḷ karutiyatālum, iḷami mutal mutumivari ettuṇi maṇañceyya muṭiyumō attuṇi maṇañ ceytaṟku aṉṟu iṭamiruntatu. āyiṉum cila aracarum kīḻōrumē in niliyi mikutiyāyp payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. peṇṭirkkuk kāmanukarccip paruvam paṉṉīrāṇṭu mutal nāṟpatāṇṭuvari eṉpatu nūṉmarapu.
peṇṇiṉ aintāṇṭu mutal nāṟpatāṇṭu varippaṭṭa paruvattip piṉvarumāṟu eḻuniliyāka ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ vakuttukkūṟum.
(1) pēti	 5- 7āṇṭu	(5) arivi	20-25 āṇṭu
(2) petumpi	 8-11 āṇṭu	(6) terivi	26-31 āṇṭu.
(3) maṅki	12-13 āṇṭu	(7) pēriḷampeṇ	32-40 āṇṭu
(4) maṭanti	14-19 āṇṭu
aracarkkuc celvacciṟappu, kīḻōrukkut tīrvi muṟiyum maṉiviyar uḻippum, palmaṇañ ceytaṟkut tuṇiyāyiruntaṉa.
akkālat taracar kaṇkaṇṭa teyvaṅkaḷātaliṉ, avar paṉmaṇañ ceytalit taṭuppār evarumilar. ātalāl, avar vaḻimuṟi  maṇaṅkaṭkuk kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭa vēṇṭuvatilli. kīḻōr, nōyum mātappūppum caṇṭiyum
pōlac ciṟu kūṭṭattaṭi ēṟpaṭiṉum, atik kāraṇamākak kāṭṭi maṟumaṇañ ceytukoḷvar. mutal maṉivikku ōrāṇṭu piḷḷiyillā viṭiṉ, uṭaṉē maṟumaṇañ ceytukoḷvatu cilar iyalpu. cilar, piḷḷi yiruppiṉum, irupālum illiyeṉṟum palarilliyeṉṟum collik koṇṭu, maṟumaṇañ ceyvatuṇṭu. itu iṉṟum toṭarntu varukiṉṟatu.
3. maṇa naṭimuṟi
paṇṭit tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu naṭantu vantaṉa:
(1) maṇappēccu
potuvāka, ōr iḷiñaṉukkup patiṉeṭṭāṇṭu nirampiya piṉ, avaṉ peṟṟōr avaṉ kāmak kuṟippaṟintō tāmākak karutiyō, perumpālum marapuppaṭi otta kulattil oru peṇṇip pēci maṇañ ceytuvippar.
ammāṉ makaḷum atti makaḷumpōla muṟikārap peṇṇāyiṉ `urimippeṇ' eṉṟum, vēṟu celvar vīṭṭup peṇṇāyiṉ `perumip peṇ' eṉṟum aḻippatu vaḻakkam. urimip peṇ illāviṭattum avaḷi maṇavāviṭattum, ayalilēyē maṇam pēcap peṟum.
maṇappēccu muṉkālattil oru tolliyāṉa kaliyāka iruntu
vantatu. maṇamakaṉ peṟṟōr, maṇakkat takka oru peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōriṭam, vaḷḷuvaṉ allatu kaṇiyaṉ kuṟitta naṉṉāḷil, periyōri viṭuppar. peṟṟōrum uṭaṉ celvatuṇṭu. peṇ pēcac cellum pōtu, kuṟi (cakuṉam), naṟcol (vāyppuḷ), puḷ (paṟavinimittam) mutaliyaṉa pārttal vaḻakkam.
peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr tam makaḷit tara icintapiṉ maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭār maṇamakaṉukkum peṇṇukkumuḷḷa poruttaṅkaḷik kaṇiya (cōtiṭa) muṟippaṭi palvēṟu vakiyiṟ pārppar.
apporuttaṅkaḷ, potuvāka,
(1) nāḷ (naṭcattiram)	(6) ōri (irāci)
(2) kaṇam	(7) kōḷ (irāciyatipati)
(3) eṇṇikki (mākēntiram)	(8) vaciyam
(4) peṇ nīṭci (stirī tīrkkam)	(9) iṇakkam (vēti)
(5) piṟavi (yōṉi)	(10) caraṭu (iraccu)
eṉap pattu vakippaṭum. ip pattu vakiyum oruṅkē perumpālum poruntuvatilli. ataṉāl, palvēṟu peṇṇi nāṭip palvēṟu ūrkkuc celvatu vaḻakkam. itu mikunta aliccaliyum kālak kaṭappiyum uṇṭu paṇṇum. itaṉālēyē, oru maṇattiṟku ēḻu cerupput tēyavēṇṭum eṉṉum paḻamoḻi eḻuntatu. iṉi, mēṟkūṟiya patiṉ poruttattuṭaṉ, vāḻnāḷ, kuṟippu (pāvakam), maram, puḷ, kulam eṉṉum aimporuttam pārppatumuṇṭu.
(2) maṇavuṟuti (niccayārttam) 
yārēṉum oru peṇṇiṭam patiṉ poruttamum amintiruppiṉ, allatu amintiruppatākac collappaṭiṉ, uṟuppuk kuṟiyum nōyk kuṟṟamum illāviṭattu, maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭārum peṇvīṭṭārum oru naṉṉāḷil peṇvīṭṭil palaraṟiya maṇavuṟuti ceytukoḷvar. av vuṟutic caṭaṅki uṟuti veṟṟili (niccaya tāmpūlam) eṉṟum aḻikkalām.
maṇavuṟutic caṭaṅkil, veṟṟilipākku, kuṅkumam, mañcaḷ mutaliyaṉa vikkappaṭṭa oru taṭṭil, peṇvīṭṭār kēṭṭa paricappaṇamum, peṇṇiṟku oru kūṟiyum vikkappeṟum. iru vīṭṭārum maṇappeṇṇiṟku aṇiyavēṇṭiya aṇikaḷum, tirumaṇac celavil ēṟṟukkoḷḷavēṇṭiya pakutikaḷum, aṉṟē pēci muṭivu ceyyappaṭum.
cila camayaṅkaḷil, maṇamakkaḷ kuṟṟaṅkuṟikaḷi maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭārum, maṇamakaḷ kuṟṟaṅkuṟikaḷi maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭārum, maṟittuvippatumuṇṭu. tirumaṇam iruvarkku vāḻvi yuṇṭupaṇṇum maṅkalanikaḻcci yātalāl, aṅṅaṉam maṟittuvippōr āyiram poy colli oru tirumaṇam ceytu vi (oru viḷakkēṟṟi vi) eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyic collit tammit tēṟṟikkoḷvar.
(3) maṇaviḻā 
i. muṉṉikaḻccikaḷ 
maṇavuṟutiyāṉa piṉ, maṇattiṟkuriya cittiri, āṉi pōṉṟa nalla mātattil, tiṅkaḷ, aṟivaṉ (putaṉ) pōṉṟa nalla kiḻamiyil, 2āvatu 7āvatu pōṉṟa nalla pakkattil (titiyil), puravi (acupati), cakaṭu (urōkaṇi) pōṉṟa nalla nāḷil (naṭcattirattil), nalla ōriyil (ilakkiṉattil) amiyum maṅkala muḻuttam (mukūrttam) kuṟikkap peṟum. ataṉpiṉ, maṇacceyti kālamum iṭamuṅ kuṟittu, uṟṟār uṟaviṉar aṉivarkkum pākku vittuc collappeṟum.
maṇamaṉi, kulamarapuppaṭi, maṇamakaṉ vīṭākavō maṇamakaḷ vīṭākavō irukkum. potuvāy, maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭil maṇam naṭipeṟum.
maṇamaṉiyil, maṇanāḷukku muṉ 3, 5, 7, 9 ākiya oṟṟitta nāḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟil, nalvēḷiyil muḻuttakkāl nāṭṭip pantal allatu koṭṭaki pōṭappeṟum. pantal alaṅkarippu avaravar celva niliyip poṟuttatu. pantalil vāḻi kamuku kūntaṟpaṉimaṭal mutaliyaṉa kaṭṭappeṟuvatuṇṭu. ettuṇi yeḷiyarāyiṉum vāḻimaram tappātu kaṭṭappeṟum. atu maṇamakkaḷ marapu kuñcuṅ kuḷuvāṉumāy vaḻivaḻi vāḻavēṇṭum eṉṉuṅ kuṟippiyuṭiyatākak karutappeṟuvatu.
pantal naṭuvil allatu ataṉ oru kōṭiyil maṇavaṟi amikkap paṭum. ataṉ kāloṉṟil aracaṅkompu kaṭṭappeṟum. ati aracāṇik
kāl eṉpar. paṇṭikkālattil aracaṉ teyvamākak karutappaṭṭatiṉālum, maṉṉaṉ uyirttē malartali yulakam eṉpatiṉālum, aracaṉik kuṟittaṟku aracaṅkompi maṇavaṟiyil naṭṭi, ati araca āṇikkāl eṉṟu aḻittatākat terikiṉṟatu. maṇavaṟiyi aracāṇi mēṭi eṉpatumuṇṭu.
maṇavaṟiyil pū, mañcaḷ, kuṅkumam, cantaṉam, arici, aṟuku, veṟṟilipākku, tēṅkāy, muḷippāliki, viḷakku, niṟikumpam, kōlappāṉi mutaliya maṅkalap poruḷkaḷ vikkappeṟum. kōlap pāṉiyi āyiraṅkaṇṇup pāṉiyeṉṟum, aracāṇip pāṉiyeṉṟum āyirattāḻiyeṉṟuñ colvatuṇṭu. maṇamakkaḷ kuṟiviṉṟi maṅkalamāy nīṭūḻi vāḻavēṇṭumeṉpatē, maṇavaṟiyil maṅkalap poruḷkaḷi vippatiṉ karuttu. viḷakkut teyvac ciṉṉam.
maṇaviṉikaḷ toṭaṅkumuṉpē, maṇamakaṉukkum maṇamakaḷukkum, muṟiyē, valakkiyilum iṭakkiyilum kāppunāṇ kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. maṇaviḻā muṭiyumvari maṇamakkaṭkup pēyālum piṟavaṟṟālum evvakit tīṅkum nērakkūṭāteṉpatē, ataṉ nōkkam. kāppu tīṅku varāmaṟ kāttal. mutaṟkālattil kuḷicampōṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa kāppunāṇē, piṟkālattil ap peyaruḷḷa aṇiyāka māṟiṟṟu.
maṇanāḷil maṇamaṉiyil maṅkala maṇamuḻā muḻaṅkum. uṟṟārum uṟaviṉarum, maṇappantaṟkīḻ vantamarvar. tirumaṇa āciriyaṉ muḻutta vēḷiyil maṇavaṟiyil vantamarvāṉ. avaṉ tamiḻap pārppāṉākavō, kulattalivaṉākavō iruppāṉ. maṇamakkaḷum nīrāṭi mañcaḷ tōytta allatu tōyāta puttāṭi yaṇintu, maṇamakaṉ muṉṉum maṇamakaḷ piṉṉumāka, taṉittaṉiyāka maṇavaṟikkuk koṇṭu varappaṭuvar. maṇamaṉi maṇamakaṉ vīṭāyiṉ maṇamakaḷum, maṇamakaḷ vīṭāyiṉ maṇamakaṉum, vēṟōr maṉiyiliruntu mēḷa tāḷattuṭaṉ aḻittuvarappeṟuvar.
ii. karaṇam 
tirumaṇa āciriyaṉ teyva vaḻipāṭāṟṟi, maṇamakkaḷ peyarum maṇamum viḷampi, viḷakkuc cāṉṟāka avarkaḷic cūḷiṭuvittu, kuṅkumam mañcaḷuṭaṉ taṭṭiliṭṭuc cūṭaṅ koḷuttappaṭṭup periyōrāl vāḻttap peṟṟa tāliyi eṭuttu, maṇamakaṉ kiyil koṭukka, avaṉ ati vāṅkip peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil kaṭṭuvaṉ. aṉṟu muḻavu iṭiyeṉa muḻaṅkum. maṇamakkaḷmēl malarmāri poḻiyum. ataṉpiṉ maṇamakkaḷ māli māṟṟikkoḷvar. periyōr aṟukum ariciyum maṇamakkaḷmēl iṭṭu attipōl tuḷirttu, ālpōṟ paṭarntu (taḻittu), aracupōl ōṅki, aṟukupōl vērūṉṟi, mūṅkilpōṟ cuṟṟam muciyāmal vāḻntiruppīr eṉṟum, `pakavaṉaruḷāl pāṅkāyiruntu patiṉāṟum peṟṟup pallāṇṭu vāḻkeṉṟum, piṟavāṟum vāḻttuvar. itu `aṟukiṭal eṉappaṭum.
iii. piṉṉikaḻccikaḷ 
tirumaṇattiṟku vantōrkkellām tēṅkāy paḻamum pākku veṟṟiliyum cantaṉamum pakirappeṟum. maṅkala makaḷirum ciṟumiyarum avaṟṟoṭu malarum peṟuvar. ataṉpiṉ tirumaṇa avi kaliyum. āyiṉ, uṟṟār uṟaviṉarellām aṅkēyē yiruntu maṇavīṭṭārkku moyyum maṇamakkaṭku naṉkoṭiyum vaḻaṅkuvar. moyyellām ōliyil eḻutappeṟum. maṇamakkaḷ periyōr kālil viḻuntu vaṇaṅkuvar.
aṉṟu māli allatu iravu, ūrvalam nikaḻum. maṇamakkaḷ avvak kula marapuppaṭi, tērilō, pallakkilō, yāṉi kutiri mītō valam varuvar. ūrvalam muṭintu vīṭu cērntavuṭaṉ, tēṅkāyuṭittum, mañcaḷ nīrāl maṇamakkaḷ pātaṅkaḷik kaḻuviyum, ālatti yeṭuttum, kaṇṇeccil (kaṇtiruṣṭi) kaḻikkappaṭum.
tirumaṇa viruntu akkālattil vēḷikkaṇakkā yirātu, nāṭkaṇakkā yirukkum. eḷiyar oru nāḷum, celvar ēḻunāḷ variyum viruntaḷippar.
maṇamakkaḷ paruvam vantōrāyiṉ aṉṟē kūṭṭappeṟuvar.
maṇaviḻā muṭintavuṭaṉ, maṇamakkaṭkuk kāppuk kaḻaṟṟappaṭum.
maṇamāṉapiṉ, 3ām, 5ām, 7ām, 9ām nāḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟil, maṇamakkaḷ, maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭil maṇam naṭantāl maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭiṟkum, maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭil maṇam naṭantāl maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭiṟkumāka vīṭu māṟuvar. itu maṟuvīṭu (allatu maruvīṭu) pōtal eṉappaṭum. maṟuvīṭu maṇamakaḷ vīṭāyiṉ, aṅku maṇamakaṉukkuc ceyyappaṭum viruntu, maru allatu maruvu eṉap peyar peṟum.
maṉiyaṟam 
maṇaviṉikaḷellām muṭintapiṉ, kōvalaṉ kaṇṇaki pōlum celvakkuṭi maṇamakkaḷi illaṟattiṟkuriya poruḷkaḷellām iṭṭu nirappappeṟṟa oru taṉimaṉiyil iruttuvatu marapu. atu maṉiyaṟam paṭuttal eṉappaṭum.
ciṉṉāṭ ceṉṟapiṉ, maṇamakaḷ peṟṟōr maṇamakaṉ vīṭu ceṉṟu, tam makaḷ maṉiyaṟam naṭattum tiṟattip pārviyiṭuvatuṇṭu. atu vīṭu pārttal eṉappaṭum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyaṉa ellārkkum potuvāṉa paruviṉic caṭaṅkukaḷ. iṉi, avvak kulamarapiṟkēṟpac ciṟappāka naṭipeṟum nuṇviṉic caṭaṅkukaḷ ettaṉiyō pala. virivañci avi viṭukkappaṭṭuḷa.
paṇṭikkāla vatuvimaṇam paṟṟiya iru pāṭṭukaḷ varumāṟu:
vāyil maṟutta tōḻikkut talimakaṉ colliyatu.
uḻuntutalip peyta koḻuṅkaḷi mitavi
peruñcōṟ ṟamali niṟpa nirikāl
taṇperum pantart tarumaṇal ñemiri
maṉiviḷak kuṟuttu māli toṭarik 
kaṉiyiruḷ akaṉṟa kaviṉpeṟu kālik	(5)
kōḷkāl nīṅkiya koṭuveṇ ṭiṅkaḷ
kēṭil viḻuppukaḻ nāḷtali vanteṉa
uccik kuṭattar puttakal maṇṭiyar
potucey kampali mutucem peṇṭir
muṉṉavum piṉṉavum muṟimuṟi tarattarap	(10)
putalvaṟ payanta titaliyav vayiṟṟu
vāliḻi makaḷir nālvar kūṭik
kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpala utavip
peṟṟōṟ peṭkum piṇiyi yākeṉa
nīroṭu corinta īritaḻ alari	(15)
palliruṅ katuppiṉ nelloṭu tayaṅka
vatuvi naṉmaṇaṅ kaḻinta piṉṟik
kalleṉ cummiyar ñerēreṉap pukutantu
pēriṟ kiḻatti yākeṉat tamartara
ōriṟ kūṭiya uṭaṉpuṇar kaṅkul	(20)
koṭumpuṟam vaḷiik kōṭik kaliṅkat
toṭuṅkiṉaḷ kiṭanta ōrpuṟan taḻīi
muyaṅkal viruppoṭu mukamputi tiṟappa
añciṉaḷ uyirtta kāli yāḻaniṉ
neñcam paṭarnta teñcā turiyeṉa	(25)
iṉṉaki irukkip piṉyāṉ viṉavaliṉ
ceñcūṭ ṭoṇkuḻi vaṇkātu tuyalvara
akamali uvakiyaḷ āki mukaṉikut
toyyeṉa iṟiñci yōḷē māviṉ
maṭaṅkoḷ matiiya nōkkiṉ	(30)
oṭuṅkīr ōti māa yōḷē.	(akam. 86)   
itaṉ poruḷ
1-4. uḻuttam paruppoṭu cērttuc camitta koḻumiyāṉa    kuḻinta poṅkalōṭu periya cōṟṟuttiraḷiyi uṇṭal iṭiyaṟātu nikaḻa, variciyāṉa kālkaḷiyuṭiya kuḷirnta periya pantaṟkīḻk koṇṭuvantu koṭṭiya maṇalip parappi vīṭṭil viḷakkēṟṟi, mālikaḷit toṅkaviṭṭu.
5-10. tīya kōḷkaḷiṉ toṭarpu nīṅkiya vaḷinta veṇṇilāvik kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟanta pukaḻiyuṭiya cakaṭam (urōkaṇi) eṉṉum nāḷ aṭiya, mikunta iruḷ nīṅkiya aḻaku poruntiya viṭiyaṟkāliyil, uccantaliyiṟ kuṭattiyum kiyil putiya akaṉṟa montiyiyum uṭiya, maṇañceytu vikkum āravāramuḷḷa mutiya maṅkala makaḷir muṉṉē taruvaṉavaṟṟiyum piṉṉē taruvaṉavaṟṟiyum muṟippaṭi eṭutteṭuttuk koṭukka, 
11-16. makaṉip peṟṟa tēmaluḷḷa aḻakiya vayiṟṟiṉiyum tūya aṇikaḷiyum uṭiya makaḷir nālvar kūṭi niṉṟu, kaṟpiṉiṉṟum tavaṟātu pala naṟpēṟukaḷippeṟṟu, uṉ kaṇavaṉ virumpip pēṇum viruppattiṟ kiṭamākuka eṉṟu vāḻtti, nīrōṭu cērttup peyta kuḷirnta itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya pūkkaḷ, aṭarnta kariyakūntalil nellōṭu viḷaṅka,
17. nalla vatuvi maṇam muṭinta piṉpu.
18-20. cuṟṟattār āravāra ōciyuṭaṉ virintu vantu, periya maṉikkiḻattiyāvāy eṉṟu collic cērttu vikka, ōr aṟiyil uṭaṉ kūṭiya puṇarccikkuriya iravil,
21-26. mutuki vaḷittuk kōṭip puṭavikkuḷ oṭuṅkik kiṭanta pakkattic cārntu, kaṭṭiyaṇikkum viruppattuṭaṉ mukatti mūṭiyirunta āṭiyi vilakka, avaḷ accattōṭu mūccuviṭṭapōtu, uṉ uḷḷam niṉittati oḷiyātu col eṉṟu piṉpu yāṉ kēṭṭatiṉāl, iṉiya makiḻcciyōṭu irukkiyil,
27-31. māṉiṉ maṭattōṭu cerukkiyuṅkoṇṭa pārviyiyum oṭuṅkiya kuḷirnta kūntaliyum uṭiya, am māniṟattiyuṭiyavaḷ, civanta maṇikaḷ patitta viḷakkamāṉa tiraṇṭa kātaṇikaḷ kātil aciya, uḷḷam niṟinta makiḻcciyaḷāki mukattit tāḻtti eṉṉi virintu vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ.
2. uṇarppuvayiṉ vārā ūṭaṟkaṇ talimakaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuc colliyatu.
mippuṟap puḻukkiṉ neykaṉi veṇcōṟu
variyā vaṇmiyoṭu puriyōrp pēṇip
puḷḷuppuṇarn tiṉiya vākat teḷḷoḷi
aṅkaṇ iruvicumpu viḷaṅkat tiṅkaṭ
cakaṭam maṇṭiya tukaḷtīr kūṭṭattuk	(5)
kaṭinakar puṉintu kaṭavuṭ pēṇip
paṭumaṇa muḻavoṭu parūuppaṇi yimiḻa 
vatuvi maṇṇiya makaḷir vituppuṟṟup
pūkkaṇum imiyār nōkkupu maṟiya
meṉpū vākip puṉpuṟak kavaṭṭili	(10)
paḻaṅkaṉṟu kaṟitta payampamal aṟukit
taḻaṅkukural vāṉiṉ talippeyaṟ kīṉṟa
maṇṇumaṇi yaṉṉa māyitaḻp pāvit
taṇṇaṟu mukiyoṭu veṇṇūl cūṭṭit
tūvuṭip polintu mēvarat tuvaṉṟi	(15)
maḻipaṭ ṭaṉṉa maṇaṉmali pantar
iḻiyaṇi ciṟappiṟ peyarviyarp pāṟṟit
tamarnamak kītta talināḷ iraviṉ
uvarnīṅku kaṟpiṉem uyiruṭam paṭuvi
muruṅkāk kaliṅkam muḻuvatum vaḷiip	(20)
perumpuḻuk kuṟṟaniṉ piṟinutaṟ poṟiviyar
uṟuvaḷi yāṟṟac ciṟuvari tiṟaveṉa
ārva neñcamoṭu pōrvi vavvaliṉ
uṟikaḻi vāḷiṉ uruvupeyart timippa
maṟitiṟaṉ aṟiyā ḷāki oyyeṉa	(25)
nāṇiṉaḷ iṟiñci yōḷē pēṇip
parūuppaki yāmpaṟ kurūuttoṭi nīlic
curumpimir āymalar vēynta
irumpal kūntal iruḷmaṟi yoḷittē'' 	               (akam. 136)
(i - ḷ.)
1-9. neñcē kuṟṟamaṟap paruppuṭaṉ kalantu ākkiya neymikunta veṇ cōṟṟi, nīṅkāta īkit taṉmiyuṭaṉ uyarnta cuṟṟattār mutaliyōri uṇpittu, puṭkuṟi iṉitākak kūṭa, teḷḷiya oḷiyiyuṭiya aḻakiya iṭamakaṉṟa periya vāṉam kaḷaṅkamaṟa viḷaṅka, tiṅkaḷic cakaṭam kūṭiya kuṟṟamaṟṟa naṉṉāḷil, maṇamaṉiyi aḻakupaṭuttik kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭṭu, maṇa mēḷattuṭaṉ periya muracam muḻaṅka, talivikku maṇanīrāṭṭiya makaḷir, tam kūriya kaṇkaḷāl imiyātu nōkki virintu maṟiya,
10-18. melliya pūviyuṭiya vākiyiṉ aḻakaṟṟa piṉ puṟattiyuṭiya kavartta iliyi, mutiya kaṉṟu kaṟitta paḷḷattiṟ paṭarnta aṟukiṉ, iṭi muḻaṅkiya vāṉattu mutaṉ maḻikku arumpiya kaḻuviya nīlamaṇi pōlum kariya itaḻiyuṭiya pāviyotta kiḻaṅkiṭattuḷḷa kuḷirnta maṇamuḷḷa arumpuṭaṉ, cērttukkaṭṭiya veḷḷiya nūliccūṭṭi, tūya puttāṭiyāṟ poliyac ceytu, viruppattuṭaṉ kūṭi, maḻiyōci pōṉṟa maṇavōci mikunta pantalil aṇikaḷi mikutiyāy aṇintiruntatiṉāl uṇṭāṉa viyarviyi viciṟiyāl āṟṟi, avaḷ cuṟṟattār avaḷi namakkut tanta mutal nāḷ iravil,
19-26. veṟuttalillāta kaṟpōṭukūṭi eṉ uyirukku uṭampāka aṭuttavaḷ, kacaṅkāta putuppōrviyit taṉ uṭal muḻutum pōrttik koṇṭatāl, mikunta puḻukkattiyaṭinta uṉ piṟi pōṉṟa neṟṟiyil arumpiya viyarviyi, mikunta kāṟṟup pōkkumāṟu, ciṟitu tiṟa eṉṟu aṉpumikka neñcattōṭu pōrviyik kavaravē, uṟiyiṉiṉṟu uruviya vāḷippōl avaḷ uruvam veḷippaṭṭu viḷaṅka, ati maṟikkum vaki aṟiyātavaḷāki, virintu nāṇi viruppattōṭu vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ.
attakiya pēraṉpuṭiyōḷ iṉṟu yām paṇimoḻi kūṟivēṇṭām uṇarātu ūṭukiṉṟaṉaḷ; ātalāl ivaḷ namakku yāraḷō?
kuṟippu : mippaṟap puḻukki eṉṟum pāṭam. puḻukki - avittu.
vāṉūr matiyañ cakaṭaṇiya eṉṟu cilappatikārattum vantiruppatāl, tiṅkaḷic cakaṭaṇiyum nāḷ tirumaṇattiṟkuc ciṟanta naṉṉāḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu.
muntiṉa pāṭṭiṟpōṉṟē itilum, tirumaṇanāḷaṉṟē maṇamakkaḷ kūṭṭappeṟṟār eṉṟu kuṟittiruppatik kavaṉikka.
II. iṭikkāla māṟutalkaḷ 
pirāmaṇar peruntokiyiṉarāyt tamiḻakam vantu cērnta piṉ, tamiḻar tirumaṇaṅkaḷiṟ pala māṟutalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
1. pirāmaṇap purōkitamum vaṭamoḻik karaṇamum 
pirāmaṇar tēvarvaḻi vanta nilattēvar (pūcurar) eṉṟum; avar muṉṉōr moḻiyākiya vētamoḻiyum ataṉoṭu vētakāla intiya vaṭṭāramoḻikaḷākiya pirākirutaṅ kalanta ilakkiya moḻiyākiya camaṟ kirutamum, tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum; iru peruntavaṟāṉa karuttukaḷ kaṭiccaṅka kālattilēyē aracar uḷḷattil āḻa vērūṉṟiviṭṭataṉāl, piṟkālattil pirāmaṇap purōkitam tamiḻakattil virintu paravuvataṟku mikunta vacatiyāyiruntatu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ palyākacāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻutiyum, cōḻaṉ irācacūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷiyum, cēraṉ palyāṉic celkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉum pōṉṟa tamiḻ vēntarum piṟa ciṟṟaracarum, pirāmaṇari nilattēvariṉum mēlākat tēvareṉṟēkoṇṭu, avar coṉṉati yellām nampi avar ēviyatiyellām iyaṟṟi, vaṭamoḻi mantiram variyiṟanta valimiyuḷḷateṉṟum, iṟivaṉ cevikku elliyaṟṟa iṉpantaruvateṉṟum, āriya vēḷvikaḷellām tappātu oruvaṉi uyarkatikkuc celuttum eṉṟum, teyvattaṉmiyuḷḷa vaṭamoḻi mantirattit teyvattaṉmiyuḷḷa pirāmaṇaṉē ōtavallāṉeṉṟum, piṟar ati ōtiṉāl ataṉ āṟṟal keṭumeṉṟum, tamiḻaruḷ uyarntōrmaṭṭum atik kātāṟ kēṭkavum ataṉāṟ payaṉpeṟavun takkavar eṉṟum, tamiḻmakkaḷ nampumāṟu ceytuviṭṭaṉar. maṉṉaṉ eppaṭi maṉṉuyir appaṭi aṉṟō!
pirāmaṇar mutalāvatu ciṟu tokiyiṉarāyiruntatiṉāl, araca riṭattumaṭṭum āriyakkaraṇamum caṭaṅkum āṟṟivantaṉar. piṉpu caṟṟut tokimikkapiṉ, peruñcelvarāṉa peru vaṇikarkkum āṟṟivantaṉar. ataṉ piṉ, toki mikamikac ciṟuvaṇikarkkum uyar vēḷāḷarkkum āṟṟat talippaṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl, perumpālum ovvoru pērūrilum oru pirāmaṇaṉ kuṭiyamara allatu amarttappaṭa nērntatu. ōr ūrkkup potuvāyiruntu av vūriluḷḷa uyarntōr caṭaṅkukaḷiyellām āṟṟi vanta pirāmaṇaṉ, ūrppārppāṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. pirāmaṇaṉ kuṭiyirāta ūrc caṭaṅkukaḷi, aṭuttavūrp pārppāṉ vantu ceyvatu vaḻakkam.
āriyak karaṇattāl viḷinta tīmikaḷ
(1) tamiḻukkum tamiḻaṉukkum tāḻvu 
vaṭamoḻi vaḻipāṭṭu moḻiyum caṭaṅku moḻiyumāy vaḻakkūṉ ṟiyapiṉ, tamiḻ avaṟṟiṟkut takātateṉṟu taḷḷappaṭṭut taṉ paḻan talimiyi iḻantatuṭaṉ, tāḻvum aṭintatu. vaṭacoṟkaḷic coṉṉāl uyarvum teṉcoṟkaḷic coṉṉāl tāḻvum uṇṭākumeṉṟa tavaṟāṉa karuttu, tamiḻmakkaḷ uḷḷattiṟ pukuntatiṉāl, vaṭacoṟkaḷ ovvoṉṟāy vaḻakkiṟ pukuntu, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tūya tamiḻccoṟkaḷi vaḻakku vīḻttiviṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻukku nērnta tāḻvu tamiḻaṉukkum nērntatu. ataṉāl, pirāmaṇaṉ toṭṭati ellāt tamiḻarum uṇṇalāmeṉṟum, ettuṇi uyarnta tamiḻaṉāyiṉum, avaṉ toṭṭatip pirāmaṇaṉ uṇṇak kūṭāteṉṟum kūṭṭaravu (camutāya) ēṟpāṭukaḷ eḻuntaṉa. itu tamiḻaṉ poruḷiyal vāḻvi mikamikat tākkiṟṟu.
cila tūya tamiḻkkulattār, tamiḻaṉ uyarviyum varalāṟṟiyum aṟiyātu, tammic cattiriyareṉṟum viciyareṉṟum collit tamakku āriyat toṭarpu kōravum, cila uyar vēḷāḷar tammic caṟcūttirar eṉṟaḻittu, tammi uyarttuvatupōṟ karutikkoṇṭu uṇmiyil tāḻttavum, nilimi ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatu.
tamiḻaruḷ uyarntavarākak karutappeṟum nelli maravūṇ (civa) vēḷāḷarum pirāmaṇarukkuk kīḻppaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāl, tamiḻarellārum tāḻttappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉar. iṉṟu tāḻttappaṭṭavar eṉappaṭuvār uṇmiyil oṭukkappaṭṭavarē.
(2) tamiḻap pārppārukkup piḻippiṉmi 
pirāmaṇap purōkitar, uyarntōriṉ allatu uyarttappaṭṭōriṉ, ellā mataviyaṟ caṭaṅkuḷiyum āṟṟum takutiyi muṟṟūṭṭākap peṟṟuviṭṭatiṉāl, tamiḻappārppār palarkkup piḻippillātu pōyiṟṟu.
(3) ciṟuteyva vaḻipāṭu 
tamiḻaruḷ uyarntōr, ciṟuteyva vaṇakka niliyum perunteyva vaṇakka niliyum kaṭantu, muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷ vaṇakka niliyi aṭintavar. aṅṅaṉamiruppavum, iṉṟu āriyak karaṇattāl katiravaṉ, tiṅkaḷ, varuṇaṉ, tī mutaliya iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷiyum kōḷkaḷiyum pūtaṅkaḷiyum, vaṇaṅkum, muntiyal nilimikkuk koṇṭuvarappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉar.
(4) kaṟpiḻukkak kūṟṟu 
pirāmaṇap purōkitaṉ, maṇamakaṉi nōkki, nī mutal nāḷ tiṅka ḷukkum (cōmaṉukkum), iraṇṭām nāḷ yāḻōrukkum (kantaruvarukkum), mūṉṟām nāḷ tīkkum (akkiṉikkum) uṉ maṉiviyi aḷittuviṭṭu nālām nāḷ avaḷi nukarvāyāka eṉṟu kūṟum karaṇavurip pakuti,
teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuteḻuvāḷ
peyyeṉap peyyum maḻi	(kuṟaḷ. 55)
eṉṟu koḷḷum tamiḻaṉ kaṟpuṇarccikku mikamika iḻukkuttaruva toṉṟām. atilum tiṅkaḷ nukarntuviṭṭu yāḻōrkkut tara, yāḻōr nukarntuviṭṭut tīkkut tara, tī nukarttuviṭṭu uṉakkut tara, nī avaḷip peṟṟu nukarvāyāka eṉṟu maṇamakaḷmēṟ piṟarkku muṉṉurimiyum maṇamakaṉukkup piṉṉurimiyun tōṉṟak kūṟuvatu, evvakiyiṉum ēṟṟukkoḷḷat takkataṉṟu.
vittuvacaṉa kōlākalaṉ eṉṉum ākkiyāḻvāṉukkum āḷavantārk kum kulōttuṅkaccōḻaṉ (?) avikkaḷattil nikaḻnta tarukkattil, āḷavantār mēṟkūṟiya kaṟpiḻukkak kūṟṟic cāṉṟu kāṭṭi, cōḻaṉ tēvi kaṟpiḻantavaḷ eṉṟu nāṭṭiyapōtu, aracaṉum araciyum uṭpaṭa aviyōr aṉivarum pārāṭṭiyatu, tamiḻaṉukku aḻiyāp paḻiyit taruvatām.
(5) vīṇ caṭaṅkukaḷ 
tī valañceytal, ammi mitittal, aruntati kāṭṭal mutaliya pala caṭaṅkukaḷ vīṇāṉaviyākum.
ammi mitittal, akaliki cāvakkatiyi niṉippittu maṇamakaḷi eccarikkum caṭaṅkākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. ammiyit tirumakaḷ taṅkum poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟākak koḷvatiṉāl, ati mitikkum vaḻakkam iyalpākat tamiḻarkkilli. atu kaṭṭukkaḻuttiyiṉ aṭiyāḷameṉṟu tamiḻap peṇṭir kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ammiyum kuḻaviyum tāyiyuñ cēyiyuṅ kuṟittu maṇamakaḷ makappēṟṟi muṉṉōkkuvaṉa veṉṟum, cilar kūṟuva tuṇṭu. eṅṅaṉamiruppiṉum, camiyaṟkiṉṟiyamiyāta ammi u ṇavut taṭṭaṟṟa nalvāḻvik kuṟippateṉṟu koḷvatu kuṟṟamākātu. ētōvoru naṟkuṟippiṉatāka maṇavaṟiyiṟ koṇṭuvantu vikkappeṟum ammikkum, aḻakiya kōlamiṭappeṟum āyiraṅkaṇṇup pāṉikkum, poruttamāka ōr āriyattoṭarpukati kaṭṭappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. akaliki cāvattik kuṟippataṟkup potuvakiyāṉa kal pōtumē! avaḷ kallākac cāvikkap paṭṭāḷ eṉṟallātu, ammiyākac cāvikkappaṭṭāḷ eṉṟu kati kūṟavilliyē! mēlum, koṅkuvēḷāḷar tirumaṇattil maṇamaṉikku veḷiyē ammi niṟuttappaṭṭu, nāṭṭukkal eṉap peyarpeṟṟup pūciyuṭaṉ vaṇaṅkappeṟukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, pirāmaṇar tirumaṇattil, maṇamakaḷ oru kalli mitikkumpōtu, maṇamakaṉ ō peṇṇē! ik kalli miti. itippōl uṟutiyāyiru. uṉakkut tīṅku ceyya nāṭuvāri aḻittuviṭu. uṉ pakivari vel eṉṟu maṇamakaḷi nōkkik kūṟuvatum, iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
aruntati kāṭṭal eṉpatum āriyat toṭarpu karutiyatē. tamiḻa marapuppaṭi aruntati taliyāya kaṟparaci yallaḷ. avaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉākiya vaciṭṭamuṉivaṉ oḻukkattippaṟṟi aiyuṟṟu, avaṉāl viṇmīṉākac cāvikkappaṭṭāḷ eṉṟu kati kūṟukiṉṟatu. tamiḻap pattiṉip peṇṭirō, kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu coṟṟiṟam pāmi, eṉṉuṅ koḷkiyiṉar. ātalāl, ātimantiyār, pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ tēviyār, kaṇṇakiyār, āriyappaṭi kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ tēviyār, tilakavatiyār mutaliya ettuṇiyō taliyāya tamiḻakak kaṟput teyvaṅkaḷirukkavum, avari viṭṭuviṭṭu aruntatiyi niṉippittal, kaṉiyiruppak kāykavarn taṟṟē.
6) karaṇam viḷaṅkāmi 
makkaḷ āṟaṟivuṭiyār. ataṉāl etic ceyyiṉum aṟivōṭu ceytaṟkuriyar. oruvar vāḻkkiyil taliciṟanta nikaḻcciyāyum, iruvar iṉpa vāḻviṟku aṭikōluvatāyum, palar makiḻcciyuṟutaṟkuriya kāṭciyāyum, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ vantu kūṭuvatāyum, uḷḷa tirumaṇaccaṭaṅkip purōkitaṉukkaṉṟip poruḷoṭu ōtu kiṉṟāṉā poruḷillātu uḷaṟu
kiṉṟāṉā eṉpatiyum aṟiya viyalāta niliyil, iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, kuruṭṭuttaṉamāka naṭattivaruvatu, nākarikattiṟkum paṇpāṭṭiṟkum muṟṟum muraṇāṉatām.
2. kulakkaṭṭuppāṭṭu miki 
toḻilpaṟṟiya kulappākupāṭu toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻakattil iruntuvantatēṉum, tamiḻa iṉam oṟṟumi kulintu ciṉṉapiṉṉamāyc cititaṟkum, ayalār eḷitāyp paṭiyeṭuttuvantu kippaṟṟutaṟkum, vaḻivakuttatu, piṟkālattil piṟappoṭu toṭarpupaṭuttappaṭṭa varuṇācirama tarumam eṉṉum āriyamuṟik kulappiriviṉiyāl viḷinta kulaveṟiyē. itaṉālēyē, kuraṅkāṉālum kulattilē koḷ. paḻaṅkālit tūrkkātē, putukkāli veṭṭātē eṉap pala tīya koḷkikaḷ piṟantaṉa. tiri kaṭalōṭiyun tirumikat tēṭu eṉṉum tāḷāṇmi mikka nāṭṭil, kaṭal tāṇṭakkūṭāteṉṟum, āṟu tāṇṭakkūṭāteṉṟum, kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
orē kulattiṟkuḷ tirumpattirumpa maṇañ ceytu vantatiṉāl aṟivāṟṟal mikka piḷḷikaḷ arukip piṟantaṉa.
aṇṭāmi, tīṇṭāmi, kāṇāmi mutaliya kūṭṭaravuk koṭumi kaḷellām, iv iṭikkālattiltāṉ talivirittut tāṇṭavamāṭiṉa.
3. poruntā maṇamum vīṇcaṭaṅkum 
kulaveṟi mella mellac cuṟṟaveṟiyi uṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟu. oru muṟikārap peṇṇukku allatu āṇukku, eṅkē vēṟiṭattil maṇam muṭintuviṭukiṉṟatō eṉṉum accattiṉāl, piḷḷip paruvattilēyē avarkku maṇam ceytu vippatu vaḻakkamāyviṭṭatu. cilar toṭṭiṟ kuḻantikkum maṇañ ceytu vittatuṇṭu. atu toṭṭil maṇam eṉṉappaṭṭatu. orutti kaṉṉiyāy iṟantāl tīkkatiyaṭivāḷ eṉṉum āriyakkoḷkiyum, iḷami maṇattiṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟṟu.
āṟiluñ cāvu nūṟiluñ cāvu ātalāl, maṇañceytu vikkappaṭṭa piḷḷi maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷ pūppaṭiyumuṉ iṟantuviṭuvatumuṇṭu. avaṉāl maṇakkappaṭṭa ciṟumi uṇmiyil avaṉi maṇantirāviṭṭālum, maṇantatupōṉṟē karutappaṭṭuk kimpeṇṇāyviṭuvāḷ. kimpeṇ maṇañceyyuṅ kulamāyiṉ, avaṭku maṇavāḻkkiyuṇṭu. atilum putup
peṇmiyi iḻantēyiruppāḷ. kimpeṇ maṇañceyyāk kulamāyiṉ, avaṭku maṇavāḻkki illavēyilli. kālamellām amaṅkaliyāka, pūppaṭintapiṉ kāmavuṇarcci yaṭakkum kariyaṟṟa tuṉpattiṟkō karutappaṭṭu, oḻukkakkēṭṭiṟkō uriyavaḷāvāḷ. piṟa peṇṭirpōl oru kaṇavaṉoṭu kūṭi vāḻavum, pūccūṭavum, maṅkala viṉikaḷiṟ kalantukoḷḷavum, veḷippaṭiyāyp piḷḷi peṟavum, iṉpanukaravum, urimiyaṟṟavaḷāy, taṉ vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum ulaka vāḻkkiyi oru ciṟivāḻkkiyum aḷaṟṟu (naraka) vāḻkkiyumākavē kaḻittu, colloṇāta tuṉpuṟuvāḷ.
piḷḷi maṇamakaṉ taṉ ciṟu maṉivi pūppaṭintu taṉṉoṭu kūṭivāḻumvari iṟavātiruppiṉum, nīṭittuvāḻum nilimiyillākkāl, mēṟkūṟiya tuṉpaṅkaḷē nērum. pūppaṭintapiṉṉarē makaḷirkku maṇañ ceytuvippiṉ, ittakiya tuṉpaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu perumpālum tappiyuyvar.
piḷḷip paruvattil maṇañceytu vippatāl, maṉivi pūppaṭinta vuṭaṉ kaṇavaṉoṭu kūṭivāḻa nērum. uṭal muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭiyumuṉ karukkoḷḷum makaḷir, talimakap pēṟṟil iṟantuviṭuvatumuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉam iṟavātiruppiṉum, piṟakkum piḷḷi uraṉuḷḷatāyum nīṭu vāḻiyāyum iruppatilli; tāyuṭalum taḷarntuviṭuvatuṇṭu;
pūppaṭiyumuṉ maṇañceytu vittālum pūppaṭintavuṭaṉ maṇañceytu vittālum, makaḷiriṉ iḷamiyip poṟutta variyil, iraṇṭum oṉṟē. pūppaṭintavuṭaṉ ellā makaḷirkkum kāmavuṇarcci ēṟpaṭṭuviṭuvatilli. itaṉāl, maṇam nikaḻnta piṉpum kūṭṭanāḷ taḷḷivikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu cērvu maṇam (cānti kaliyāṇam) allatu cērvu muḻuttam (cānti mukūrttam) eṉṉum vīṇ caṭaṅkiṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟṟu.
kāmak kuṟippum cērkkit tuṇivum illāta iḷamparuva āṇiṟkum peṇṇiṟkum maṇañceytu vippatiṉālēyē, avaṟṟi avarkku ūṭṭumporuṭṭu, nalaṅkiṭal, uṇavūṭṭal, veṟṟili maṭittuk koṭuttal, appaḷantaṭṭal, oṟṟiyā iraṭṭiyā piṭittal, pūcceṇṭeṟital, eḻuttāṇiyeṭuttal, oḷintu viḷiyāṭal, mōtiram eṭuttal, piḷḷiyār allatu pommi koṭuttal, ūñcalāṭal, kōpittukkoṇṭu pōṉa maṇamakaṉi aḻittuvaral, mañcaḷ nīrāṭal mutaliya pala vīṇāṉa caṭaṅkukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. maṇavāḻkkikkēṟṟa vaḷarcciyaṭinta paruvattil makkaṭku maṇañceytu vittāl, avar piṟar tuṇiyiṉṟit tāmākavē kūṭi vāḻvar.
cila tamiḻakkulattār maṇavaṟiyil tirikaṭṭit tāli kaṭṭuvatu, makamatiyar in nāṭṭiṟku vantapiṉ ēṟpaṭṭa payaṉaṟṟa ceyalākum.
iṉi, vaḷarcciyaṭinta makkaṭku maṇañceytu vittaviṭattum, avar viruppattik kēḷātu peṟṟōr tam viruppappaṭiyē iciyāta iḷiñaṉiyum iḷiñiyiyum iṇittu vittu, piṉpu avar tuṉpuṟumpōtu tāramuṅ kuruvun taliviti eṉṟum, kaḻuttil viḻunta māli kaḻaṟṟa muṭiyātu eṉṟum, aṭuppil vitta koḷḷi eṟintutāṉ tīravēṇṭum eṉṟum, kūṟuvatu avar maṭamiyik kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvatē yākum.
iṉi maṇamakkaḷiṉ otta vaḷarcciyi oruciṟitum kavaṉiyātu, oru ciṟuvaṉukku oru perum peṇṇi maṇañceytu vittu māmaṉē avaḷukkuk kaṇavaṉāvatum, oru muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭinta muraṭaṉik kaṇṭu añcic cākumāṟu, avaṉukku oru maṅkip paruvattu melliyaḷi maṇam puṇarppatum, iṭikkālattu nikaḻntuvanta koṭuñ ceyalkaḷām.
paḻañcēra nāṭākiya maliyāḷa nāṭṭil, pūppiṉmuṉ tālikaṭṭu eṉṉum poy maṇamum, pūppiṉpiṉ campantam eṉṉum meymmaṇamum, āka irumaṇam ovvoru peṇṇiṟkum cila kulattil nērntataṟkuk kāraṇam āriyamē.
III. tirumaṇac cīrtiruttam
1. cīrtirutta iyakkam
iṭikkālattil tamiḻ marapiṟkovvāta pala āriya muṟikaḷum caṭaṅkukaḷum tamiḻ maṇaṅkaḷiṟ kalantuviṭṭataṉālum, avaṟṟāl tamiḻukkum pala tīmikaḷ viḷintatiṉālum, avaṟṟik kaḷintu tamiḻ maṇattit tiruttaṟkup pala cīrtiruttat talivar tōṉṟiṉar. avaruḷ nāyakamāṉavar nālvar. avarāvār :
(1) cittūr mārkkacakāya āccāriyār
ivar ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil cittūr māvaṭṭattil tōṉṟiyavar; poṟkollar vakuppiṉar; vaṭamoḻi vēta itikāca purāṇaṅkaḷi naṉṟāyk kaṟṟavar. ivar, kammāḷar vicuvappirāmaṇar eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi vētappaṭi vicuvappirāmaṇarē tirumaṇam naṭattivikkavēṇṭumeṉṟum, pirāmaṇar makkaḷi ēmāṟṟit toṉṟutoṭṭu naṭantuvanta muṟiyiṉi iṭiyil māṟṟiviṭṭaṉareṉṟum, pōrāṭi, oru tirumaṇatti vaṭamoḻiyil naṭatti vikkat toṭaṅkiyapōtu, pañcāṅkam kuṇṭiyar eṉṉum pirāmaṇap purōkitar vantu taṭuttār. mārkkacakāya āccāriyār avarukku iṇaṅkāta tiṉāl kalakam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ataṉ viḷivāy oru naṭuvar kuḻuk kūṭiṟṟu. ataṉ muṉṉiliyil, pañcāṅkam kuṇṭiyar viṉaviya viṉākkaṭkellām mārkkacakāya āccāriyār uṭaṉuṭaṉ takka viṭi viṭuttuviṭṭataṉāl, āccāriyār pakkam muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu. ataṉpiṉṉum pañcāṅkam kuṇṭiyar viṭāmal, muṟiyē, kīḻ muṟiyāḷar (Sub Magistrate) maṉṟattilum, māvaṭṭa maṉṟattilum, vaḻakkut toṭuttār. aṅkum viṉaviya vaṟṟiṟkellām takkavāṟu viṭuttuviṭṭataṉāl, āccāriyār pakkamē tīrppāyiṟṟu. ip pōrāṭṭamum veṟṟiyum, cittūr atālattuk kōrṭṭut tīrppu eṉṉum talippil puttaka vaṭivāy veḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷa.
(2) maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ 
ivar taṉittamiḻ iyakkat talivarātalāl, āriya moḻiyi aṟavē akaṟṟi, tirumaṇak karaṇamoḻi muḻuvatiyum tūya tamiḻil amittuk koṇṭār. vēḷvi vaḷarttalum tīvalam varutalum maṇavaṟiyil maṅkalap poruḷ vittalum, ivarāl vilakkappaṭavilli.
(3) tiru.vi.kaliyāṇacuntaraṉār 
ivarum maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ pōṉṟārē. āyiṉ, aṭikaḷ karaṇa moḻiyil maruntiṟkum vaṭacol kāṇpataritu; ivar moḻiyilō orōvōr iṭattil vaṭacoṟ kalakkum. ituvē ivartammuḷ vēṟṟumi.
eṉiṉum, piṟarmoḻipōl maṇippavaḷa māliyum paṉmaṇik kōviyumā yirātu, vaṭacol arukivarap peṟuvatē maṇavaḻakaṉār (kaliyāṇacuntaraṉār) moḻiyum eṉka.
(4) ī.vē. irāmacāmip periyār 
ivar, āriyak karaṇattiyum maṇavaṟi yamippiyum kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭiyum maṇaccaṭaṅkukaḷiyum vilakki, potumakkaḷ tamiḻil vāḻkki oppantatti vakuttut tantavar. ivar ceyta māṟutalkaḷuḷ, kaṭavuḷvaḻipāṭṭu vilakku nampā matattārālēyē yaṉṟi nampumatattārāl cīrtiruttamākak koḷḷappeṟātu.
iṅṅaṉam, mārkkacakāyar pirāmaṇiyattiyum, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum kaliyāṇacuntaraṉārum aḵtuṭaṉ āriya moḻiyiyum, ī.vē.rā. avaṟṟōṭu vīṇ caṭaṅkukaḷiyum, vilakkit tirumaṇak karaṇattit tiruttiyavarāvār.
2. peṟṟōr kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyavi 
maṇamakkaḷ perumpālum ulakiyalaṟiyā iḷamiyarātalāṉum, maṇavāḻkki āyiraṅkālattup payirātalāṉum iḷami eḷitāy uṇarcci vayappaṭṭuk keṭṭuviṭutalāṉum, tirumaṇap poṟuppi muṟṟum maṇamakkaḷiṭattil viṭṭuviṭātu, peṟṟōr muṟpaṭavē viḻippākavum eccarikkiyākavumiruntu piṉvarum paṉṉiru kaṭamikaḷi āṟṟalvēṇṭum.
(1) pālnili kavaṉittal 
cila peṟṟōr, āṇuṭampu koṇṭārellām āṇmiyareṉṟum, peṇṇuṭampu koṇṭārellām peṇmiyareṉṟum karuti, avar pāliyalpu aṟṟiruppatiyum tirintiruppatiyum kavaṉippatē yilli.
āṇmi tirintu peṇmi koṇṭavaṉ pēṭi; peṇmi tirintu āṇmi koṇṭavaḷ pēṭaṉ; iv viruvarkkum potup peyar pēṭu; irupālum allātatu ali. im mūvarkkum maṇamē vēṇṭiya tilli.
iṉi, irupāluḷḷum, pāluṇarcciyō cērkki viruppamō oru ciṟitum illātavarumuṇṭu. avarkkum maṇam vēṇṭuvatilli.
āṇmi tiriyātavaruḷḷum pala tīya paḻakkaṅkaḷāl cērkkiyāṟṟal iḻantavar cilar. avarkku maṇañceytuvippiṉ, avar maṉiviyar oḻukkaṅkeṭa vēṇṭum; allatu kaṇavarkkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭuk kālamellām erintu vēkavēṇṭum.
orōvoru peṇ orupōtum pūppaṭivatilli. avarkkum maṇam vēṇṭuvatilli.
makkaḷtoki mikkuḷḷa ikkālattil, maṇaṅkaḷi ettuṇik kuṟittal muṭiyumō attuṇik kuṟittal vēṇṭum.
(2) paṉṉiru poruttam pārttal 
i. kātaṟ poruttam 
maṇamakkaṭku mutaṉmiyāka vēṇṭum poruttam ituvē. iḵtillāviṭiṉ, ēṉic ciṟappellāmiruntum ētum payaṉilli. ātalāl, maṇamakkaḷ icivu peṟṟē avar peṟṟōr avarkku maṇañceytu vittal vēṇṭum.
ii. uṭalnalap poruttam 
maṇamakkaḷ iṉpan tuyttaṟkum illaṟam naṭattaṟkum iṉṟiyami yātatu uṭalnalam. cila nōykaḷ maṟivāyirukkum allatu maṟikkap paṭṭirukkum. ātalāl, takka maruttuvap paṇṭi tarik koṇṭu maṇamakkaḷi nōṭṭañceytal vēṇṭum. allākkāl, oruvarāl iṉṉoruvar nōyppaṭuvatuṭaṉ, avarkkup piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷum vaḻivaḻi nōyppaṭṭu varuntavēṇṭiyirukkum.
iii. oḻukkap poruttam 
nalloḻukkattil tuppuravum (cuttamum) aṭaṅkum. oḻukkap poruttamillākkāl, auviyārk kamutu paṭikkac coṉṉavaṉum kuṇṭalakēciyumpōl tuṉpuṟa nērum.
iv. karuttup poruttam 
kaṭci matam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ karuttottalum, vēṟupaṭiṉ av vēṟu pāṭṭiṟku iṭantaralum karuttupporuttamām. uyarnta paṇpāṭṭiṉarāyiṉ, kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum vevvēṟu matattiṉarāyu mirukkalām.
v. uṇṭip poruttam 
maravuṇavum pulāluṇavum ākiya iraṇṭaṉuḷḷum oṉṟil ottirutta lum, ovvātu vēṟupaṭiṉ av vēṟupāṭṭiṟku iṭantaralum uṇṭip poruttamām.
vi. akavip poruttam 
maṇamakkaḷ uṭal nalvaḷarcciyaṭintapiṉ maṇantāltāṉ, naṉmakap pēṟuṇṭāvatuṭaṉ tāykkuñ cētamiṉṟām. atē camiyattil, iḷami kaḻiyumuṉ maṇantāltāṉ, iṉpacciṟappum piṟa vacatikaḷum uṇṭākum. ātalāl, piṉvarumāṟu maṇamakkaḷ akavi kavaṉikkappeṟiṉ nalamām.
	ciṟṟelli	iṭiyelli	pērelli
maṇamakaṉ akavi	20	22	25
maṇamakaḷ akavi	16	18	20
in nāṭu veppa nāṭātalāl, kuḷirnāṭukaḷiṟpōl iḷamiyar nīṇṭanāḷ maṇavātiruttal iyalātu. ātalāl, makkaḷ pērelliyakavi aṭintavuṭaṉ, peṟṟōr avarkku maṇañceytu vittal nallatu.
takka paruvattil maṇañceytu viyāmiyāl, iḷiñarum iḷiñiyarum pala tīya paḻakkaṅkaṭku āḷāyviṭukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl iḷiñar koṭiya nōykaḷi aṭivatuṭaṉ, cērkki yāṟṟaliyum iḻantuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. āṇmiyoṉṟē āṭavarkkuc ciṟappu. ataṉāl iṉpuṟun tiṟaṉmaṭṭumaṉṟi akakkaraṇa valimiyum vāḻnāḷum mikukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl, āṇmiyiḻantuviṭiṉ āṭavaṉukku vāḻkkiyilli. ataṉ
piṉ, irunitikkiḻavaṉāyiṉum īṭilā arampiyip peṟiṉum eṉṉa payaṉ?
mēlum, pinti maṇañceyvatāl, piḷḷikaḷun taliyeṭukkāmal peṟṟōriṉ mutumiyiṟ ciṉṉatuñciṟiyatumā yiruntu, kuṭumpam mikamika iṭarppaṭukiṉṟatu.
ivaṟṟiyellām nōkki, iṉimēlāyiṉum, peṟṟōr tam piḷḷikaṭkut takka paruvattil maṇañ ceytuvippārāka.
vii. uruvap poruttam 
maṇamakkaḷ vaḷarttiyilum parumaṉilum aḻakutarattilum ottiruppatu uruvap poruttamām. maṇamakaṉilum maṇamakaḷ kuṭṭiyāyum olliyāyum irukkalām. itaṟku māṟāyiruttal kūṭātu.
aḻaku iṉpatti mikuttuk kāṭṭuvatāl, aḻakuṇarcciyuḷḷa maṇamakkaṭku iyaṉṟavari aḻakuḷḷa vāḻkkittuṇiyē amarttal vēṇṭum.
maṇamakkaḷ uṟuppuk kuṟiyillātiruttalum uruvap poruttamām. cila peṟṟōr, tam makaṉiṉ allatu makaḷiṉ uṟuppuk kuṟiyi āṭiyaṇi
kaḷāl maṟittu vippatuṇṭu. ati naṉṟāyk kavaṉittal vēṇṭum.
viii. kalvip poruttam 
maṇamakkaḷ iruvarum kaṟṟōrāyirukkalām; allatu maṇamakaṉ maṭṭum kaṟṟirukkalām. maṇamakaḷ kaṟṟum maṇamakaṉ kallātum iruttal kūṭātu. maṇamakaḷ maṇamakaṉiṉum mikutiyākak kaṟṟiruttalum, cerukkiṟkum ottuḻiyāmikkum kāraṇamāyiṉ naṉṟaṉṟu.
maṇamakaḷ iṉṟiyamiyātu kaṟṟirukkak vēṇṭiya nūlkaḷ (Sciences) uṭalnūlum, (Physiology) karunūlum, (Embryology) piḷḷivaḷarppu nūlum ākum; kalikaḷ (Arts) camiyalum tiyalum iciyumākum. poruttam pārppatu maṇamakkaḷ icintu iṉitāy illaṟam naṭakkaṟporuṭṭēyātaliṉ, av icivupāṭṭiṟkuk kāraṇamāka ellā nilimiyum poruttamē.
oru maṉikkiḻattiyār ettuṇiyuyarnta kalvip paṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟṟiruppiṉum, camiyal teriyātiruppiṉ, avar kalvi niṟivuṟṟatākak koḷḷappaṭātu. kaṭṭaḻakellām iḷamik kālattiltāṉ iṉpuṟuttik kuṟi maṟikkum. iṭimiyilum mutumiyilum iṉpuṟuttuvatu aṟucuvi yuṇṭiyē. tāykkuppiṉ tāram eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. aṟucuvi yuṇṭi amarntillā ḷūṭṭa eṉpatu nālaṭiyār (1). vaḷḷuvar taputāra niliyiṟ pāṭiya kiyaṟam,
aṭiciṟ kiṉiyāḷē aṉpuṭiyāḷē
eṉṟu toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu.
maṉikkiḻattiyār araca allatu peruñ celvak kuṭiyic cērntavarāyiruntu, camiyaṟku oru maṭinūl vallāṉi  amarttik koṇṭālum, aṉṟum avarkku ak kali terintiruppatu naṉṟēyām. mālikkālattilum kaḷippunērattilum maṉiviyiṉ miṭaṟṟici allatu karuvi ici kaṇavaṟku iṉpūṭṭum.
iṉi, maṇamakkaḷ iruvarum tāymoḻiyil ottiruppatum kalvip poruttattiṉ pāṟpaṭum. moḻiyē kalvi vāyilātaliṉ, iruvar moḻiyum vēṟupaṭṭiruppiṉ, oruvar moḻiyi oruvar virintu kaṟṟukkoḷḷal vēṇṭum.
ix. muṟip poruttam
maṇamakkaḷ uṟaviṉarāyiṉ, maṇamuṟiyiṉarā yiruttal vēṇṭum. muṟiyal maṇattiṉāl (Incest) naṉmakappēṟu uṇṭāvatilli. kuṭikkum iḻukkām.
iṉi, akkiyār makaḷi maṇattal muṟimaṇamāyiṉum, neruṅkiya vuṟavāyiruttalāl atuvum vilakkaṟpālatē. kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyariṉ aratta uṟavu neruṅka neruṅka uṭal noymiyum mati maḻukkamum, akala akala uṭaluramum mati viḷakkamum, avartam makkaṭku amiyum eṉpati aṟitalvēṇṭum.
x. viṉittiṟap poruttam
maṇamakkaḷ iruvarum cūḻcci valimiyilum cuṟucuṟuppilum ottiruppatu, viṉittiṟap poruttamām. avaruḷ oruvar virimatiyum tāḷāṇmiyuṭiyavarāyirukka, iṉṉoruvar manta matiyum cōmpalu
muṭiyavarāyiruppiṉ avartam illaṟa vāḻkki oṟṟumiyum iṉpamum uṭiyatāyirātu.
xi. toḻiṟ poruttam
maṇamakkaḷ iruvarum orē toḻili allatu toṭarpuḷḷa toḻil kaḷic ceytal, toḻiṟ poruttamām. toḻil vēṟupaṭiṉum māṟupaṭal kūṭātu.
xii. kuṭumpanilip poruttam 
ūril oruvaṉē tōḻaṉ, ārumaṟṟatē tāram eṉpatālum, maṇamakaḷ celva mikkavaḷāyiṉ maṇamakaṉukku naṉṟēyātalālum, celvap poruttam ciṟappāyk kavaṉittaṟkuriya toṉṟaṉṟām. āyiṉum, iḷavaracaṉum kūlikkāriyumpōl, allatu kūlikkāraṉum iḷavaraciyumpōl, maṇamakkaḷ celvanili aḷaviṟantu vēṟupaṭiṉ, avar oruvarukkoruvar vāḻkkittuṇiyāy iṇittu vikkappaṭaṟkuriyavarākār. kuṭumpa niliyip piṟappu eṉṟu kūṟiṉum okkum.
iṅṅaṉam paṉṉiru poruttamum pārātu ceytu vikkappaṭum allatu ceyyappaṭum maṇaṅkaḷ,
āyntāyntu koḷḷātāṉ kēṇmi kaṭimuṟi
tāṉcān tuyaran tarum	 (792)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaṭ kilakkāṉa naṭpuppōl, tuṉpan taruvaṉavēyām.
piṟappē kuṭimi āṇmi yāṇṭō(ṭu)
uruvu niṟutta kāma vāyil
niṟiyē aruḷē uṇarvoṭu tirueṉa
muṟiyuṟak kiḷanta oppiṉatu vakiyē
eṉpatu, tolkāppiyam (meyppāṭṭiyal, 25) kūṟum patiṉ poruttamākum.
itaṉ poruḷ :otta  piṟappum, otta oḻukkamum, otta āṇmiyum, otta paruvamum, otta uruvaniliyum, otta aṉpum, otta niṟiyum, otta aruḷum, otta aṟivum, otta celvamum eṉat talimakaṉum talimakaḷum (kātalaṉum kātaliyum) ottirukkum pakutikaḷ pattu vakiya eṉṟavāṟu.
ivaṟṟuḷ, niṟiyum aruḷum oḻukkattuḷ aṭaṅkum.
otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum kāṇpa
mikkōṉ āyiṉum kaṭivari yiṉṟē
eṉṟu tolkāppiyak kaḷaviyal (2) kūṟuvatāl, perumpālum maṇamakkaḷ palavakiyilum ottirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatum, av oppumiyē nīṭitta iṉpavāḻkkikku ētuvāṉateṉpatum, peṟappaṭum.
(3) kulaveṟi koḷḷāmi 
tamiḻarip pirittuk keṭuttaṟkeṉṟē, piṟappoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa kulappiriviṉi ayalārāṟ pukuttappaṭṭateṉṟum, atu nūlutti paṭṭaṟivukaṭku muṟṟum muraṇāṉateṉṟum ulaka muḻumiyiṉum innāvalan tēyattilēyē atu uḷḷateṉṟum, tiruvaḷḷuvar tirumūlar mutaliya periyōrellārā lum kaṇṭikkappaṭṭateṉṟum, uṇarāta tamiḻar, iṉṉum kulavuṇarcci yilēyē tiḷittu varukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl, ovvoru kulattārum tamakkuḷḷēyē maṇantu, tamiḻiṉattil pēraṟivuc cuṭarkaḷ peruvāriyāyp piṟavātapaṭi taṭuttuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. atōṭu tamiḻarkkuḷ oṟṟumiyum illātu pōkiṉṟatu.
oṉṟupaṭṭāluṇṭu vāḻvu - iṅku
oṟṟumi yiṉṟēl aṉivarkkup tāḻvu
itittamiḻ mākāṇa muṉṉēṟṟam paṟṟiyāvatu kavaṉittal vēṇṭum.
nīṇṭakālamākat toṭarntuvarum kulamuṟiyit tiṭumeṉṟu nīkkuvatu iyalātēṉum, uyarttappaṭṭōrkkum tāḻttappaṭṭōrkkum itupōtu tuppuravu paṟṟi ēṟṟattāḻvu uḷḷatēṉum, vēḷāḷarum mutaliyārum pōlvārum, maṟavarum iṭiyarum pōlvārum, iraṭṭiyārum nāyuṭuvum pōlvarum, tammuḷ maṇantukoḷḷalāmē!
tamiḻariṉ palvēṟu peruṅkulaṅkaḷaṉṟi, (nāṭum ūrumākiya) iṭam, uṇporuḷ, uṭi, ciṟu teyvam, koḷki, caṭaṅku, ceyporuḷ, ciṟappu nikaḻcci, kuṇam, niṟam, kuṭuminili, vaḻakkam, aṇi, tālivaki, moḻi, aluval, paṭṭam, kulavarici, ciṉṉam, makkaḷ toki, tokuppu, kalappu, tōṟṟakkati, tiṟami, muṟippeyar mutaliya paṟpala vakippaṟṟip pirintu
kiṭakkum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa poruntāk kulappirivukaḷiṟ palavum, akamaṇa vakuppukkaḷākavē yiruntuvaruvatu, tamiḻaṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku māperuntaṭi
yātaliṉ, kalvikaṟṟa tamiḻa iḷiñar kuruṭṭuttaṉamum kuṟukiya nōkkumuḷḷa kulamutiyar coṟkoḷḷātu, ati virintu takarntateṟiyakkaṭavar.
(4) paricam vāṅkāmi 
peṇṇiṟkup paricam kēṭpatu ōraḷavu vili kūṟuvatu pōṉṟirutta lāl, celvap peṟṟōr ataṉik kēḷātiruttal ciṟappām. ēḻip peṟṟōrāyiṉ, maṇaccelavu nōkki oru toki kēṭpatu kuṟṟamākātu.
iṉi, peṇṇiṟkuk kēṭpataṟkup patilāka maṇavāḷap piḷḷikkuk kēṭpatu, iyaṟkikku māṟāṉatāyum, ekkāraṇattālum carimippaṭutta muṭiyātatāyum irukkiṉṟatu. ivvulakattil oruvaṉ peṟakkūṭiya pēriṉpap pēṟu peṇṇē. akattaḻakum puṟattaḻakum oruṅkēyaminta arumip peṇṇirukkavum avaḷi viṭṭuviṭṭu, kāciṟkācippaṭṭu aḻakiliyi maṇappatu, illaṟa iṉpattiyum vāḻkki vacatiyiyum paṇattiṟku viṟpatu pōṉṟatē.
(5) nāḷum vēḷiyum pārāmi 
nāḷ ceyvatu nallār ceyyār, eṉpatu nam muṉṉōriṉ koḷkiyēyāṉiṉum, aṟiviyal (viññāṉam) vaḷarcciyaṭintuḷḷa iklattiṟku atu ēṟkātu.
iṭam eṉpatu eṅṅaṉam eṅkum parantu taṉṉaḷavil vēṟupāṭaṟṟatō, aṅṅaṉamē kālam eṉpatum eṉṟum parantu taṉṉaḷavil vēṟupāṭaṟṟatām. pakaliravum avaṟṟāl nāḷum vēḷiyum ēṟpaṭuvataṟkuk kāraṇam, katiravaṉ tōṟṟamaṟivu allatu ñālattiṉ (pūmiyiṉ) cuḻaṟciyē. iṟivaṉ,
aṇṭap pakutiyiṉ uṇṭip piṟakkam
aḷapparun taṉmi vaḷapperuṅ kāṭci
oṉṟaṉuk koṉṟu niṉṟeḻil pakariṉ
nūṟṟoru kōṭiyiṉ mēṟpaṭa virintaṉa
ilnuḻi katiriṉ nuṇaṇup puriyac
ciṟiya vākap periyōṉ	(tiruvācakam, tiruvaṇṭappakuti, 1-6)
ātaliṉ, nāḷuṅkōḷum avaṉ āṇikkaṭaṅkiyē naṭakkum. avaṉaṉṟi aṇuvum aciyātu, `nāḷuṅkōḷumē nallatu ceyyum' eṉṟu nampuvār, kaṭavuḷi nampātavarum, avaratu
eṭṭut ticiyum patiṉāṟu kōṇamum eṅkumoṉṟāy
muṭṭit tatumpi muḷitteḻu cōti ttaṉmi
aṟiyātavarumē yāvar.
tiruñāṉa campantar maṅkiyarkkaraciyāriṉ vēṇṭukōṭkiṇaṅkic camaṇari yoḻikka maturi ceṉṟapōtu, iṉṟu nāḷuṅkōḷum naṉṟā yillāmiyāl tāṅkaḷ maturi cella vēṇṭā eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracar taṭukka, avar, 
 vēyuṟu tōḷipaṅkaṉ viṭamuṇṭakaṇṭaṉ mikanalla vīṇitaṭavi
 mācaṟu tiṅkaḷkaṅki muṭimēlaṇinteṉ uḷamē pukuntavataṉāl
nāyiṟu tiṅkaḷcevvāy putaṉviyāḻam veḷḷicaṉi pāmpiraṇ 				ṭumuṭaṉē
ācaṟu nallanalla avinallanalla aṭiyāra varkkumikavē''
 
eṉpoṭu kompoṭāmi ivimār pilaṅka erutēṟi yēḻi
	yuṭaṉē
poṉpoti mattamāli puṉalcūṭi vanteṉ uḷamē pukunta
	vataṉāl
oṉpato ṭoṉṟoṭēḻu patiṉeṭṭō ṭāṟumuṭaṉāya
	nāḷkaḷ avitāṉ
aṉpoṭu nallanalla avinallanalla aṭiyā ravarkku
	mikavē
eṉṟu pāṭicceṉṟu muḻu veṟṟi kaṇṭār. innilimi campantarpōlum periyārkkumaṭṭumaṉṟu, uṇmiyaṉpuḷḷa ellārkkum urittām.
nāḷaṉṟu pōkip puḷḷiṭi taṭpap
pataṉaṉṟu pukkut tiṟaṉaṉṟu moḻiyiṉum
vaṟitu peyarkuva rallar neṟikoḷap
pāṭāṉ ṟiraṅku maruvip
pīṭukeḻu maliyaṟ pāṭi yōrē      	(puṟam.124)
eṉṟu, celliṉuntappāta maliyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāriyiṉ vaḷḷaṉmiyip pukaḻntuḷḷār kapilar.
nāḷkōḷkaḷiṉ iyakkattāl maḻiyum maḻiyiṉmiyum pōla naṉmi tīmi viḷivatu uṇmiyāyiṉum, avviḷivi av iyakkat tiṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa ellām valla iṟivaṉ ēṟpāṭākak koḷvatallatu, ñālampōl uyiraṟṟa aḵṟiṇiyiṭapporuḷkaḷāya nāḷkōḷkaḷiṉ ceyalākak koḷvatu eṅṅaṉam poruntum? tamiḻnāṭṭuk kīḻikkarip puyaṟcētattāl tuṉpuṟṟa makkaṭku ceṉṉi mākāṇa mutalamiccar matitaku kāmarāca nāṭār pukivaṇṭi vāyilāy naṭippirakāram aṉuppiṉāreṉiṉ, am mutalamiccarkku naṉṟi kūṟuvatallatu appukivaṇṭikku naṉṟi kūṟuvatu poruntumō?
ovvoru naṉmuyaṟcikkum maṅkalaviṉikkum, nāḷuṅ kiḻamiyum ōriyum vēḷiyum pārttup pārttu eṉṟum accattōṭēyē vāḻvatāl, intiyar carācari vāḻnāḷ kuṟintum; avaṟṟippārāta mēṉāṭṭār vāḻnāḷ kūṭiyum uḷḷaṉa. ivvuṇmiyik kīḻvarum carācari vāḻnāṭ paṭṭiyiṟ kāṇka.
