nāḷum vēḷiyum pārppavar vāḻnāḷ kūṭiyum, pārātār vāḻnāḷ kuṟintum, iruppatiṟkup patilāka, avi nērmāṟāyiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu. intiyar vāḻnāṭ kuṟiviṟku vaṟumiyum veppamikiyum peruṅ kāraṇamēṉum, naṉṉāḷ viṉi ceykiyāl ciṟitum ataṟku īṭu ceyyap paṭāmiyi nōkkuka.
nāḷ ceyvatu nallār ceyyār eṉpatu uṇmiyāyiṉ, nalla nāḷil maṇappavarellām nīṇṭa vāḻviṉarāyum, tīya nāḷil maṇappavarellām kuṟukiya vāḻviṉarāyum, iruttalvēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamiṉmi veḷippaṭi.
mēlum, oru peruviṉi naṉṉāḷilum nalvēḷiyilum toṭaṅkap peṟiṉum, tīyanāḷilum tīyavēḷiyilum toṭarntu ceyyappaṭuvatiyum kavaṉikka.
tiruvaruḷ peṟṟōr uṭaṟkaṭṭu, nalluṇavu, uṭaṟpayiṟci, akamalarcci, kavaliyiṉmi, ciṉamiṉmi, nalloḻukkam, aṟivuṭimi, varampukaṭavāmi mutaliyavaṟṟālēyē makkaḷ vāḻnāḷ nīṭikkum eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
cittiri, vikāci, āṉi, ti, paṅkuṉi ākiya aimmātaṅkaḷē tirumaṇattiṟkuriyaṉavākak koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ, iṭiyil nikaḻakkūṭiya pala tirumaṇaṅkaḷ niṉṟuviṭukiṉṟaṉa; allatu vīṇāka niṟuttivikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
maṇavuṟuti ceyyappaṭṭa piṉpum kuṟitta kiḻamiyilum vēḷiyilum varāmiyāl maṇamakaḷiyiḻanta maṇamakaṉum, maṇamakaṉiyiḻanta maṇamakaḷum uḷar.
ākavē, uṭalnalattiyum viṉivacatiyiyum tākkum kōṭi māripōṉṟa kāla vēṟupāṭum, pakal iravu pōṉṟa vēḷi  vēṟupāṭum allatu, vēṟuvakiyiṟ kālappakutikaḷik kaṇittu vīṇāka iṭarppaṭuvati viṭṭuviṭalvēṇṭum.
nakaraṅkaḷil naṭipeṟum tirumaṇaṅkaṭku varuvār palar aluvalāḷarāyiruppārātalāl, avar vacati nōkki, potu viṭumuṟiyallāta nāḷkaḷil naṭattum tirumaṇaṅkaḷiyellām, kāli 8 maṇikku muṉṉāvatu māli 4 maṇikkup piṉṉāvatu vittukkoḷvatu nalam.
(6) piṟappiyam pārāmi 
iṉi, maṇamakkaḷiṉ piṟappiyatti (jātakatti) nōkkuvatum tavaṟām. maṉapporuttamē maṇapporuttam. aḵtaṉṟik kaṇiya muṟiyil vevvēṟu poruttam pārppatāl, poruntum maṇaṅkaḷ vilakkavum poruntā maṇaṅkaḷ poruttavum paṭukiṉṟaṉa. piṟappiyak kaṇippuppaṭi perumpāl nilimikaḷ nērvatilli. ētēṉum nēriṉum atu taṟceyalāka nērvatē. piṟappiyat tavaṟṟik kaṇipput tavaṟeṉṟu kūṟuvatum poruntātu. cariyāyk kaṇippavar evarumilar. aintiṟkiraṇṭu paḻutillātirukkum eṉpatē ekkaṇiyar kūṟṟum. avviraṇṭutāṉ eviyeṉpatum avar aṟiyār.
piṟappiyattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa vāḻnāḷ nīṭciyi nampi, nōy maruttuvam ceyyātum uṭalip pēṇātum iṟantupōṉavarum; ākkaniliyi nampi, muyaṟci ceyyātu viṉitōṟṟārum, muṟkāppiṉṟi ēṟkeṉavē irukkum nilimiyumiḻantōrum eṇṇiṟantōrāvar.
orē nāḷilum orē vēḷiyilum piṟappavarellārum orē nilimi yaṭivatiṉmiyāl, taṉ muyaṟciyiyum iṟivaṉaruḷiyum piṟar tuṇiyiyumē nampi vāḻtalvēṇṭum. itaṉāl kaṇiyar piḻippuk keṭātavāṟu, avari uṭuniliyaṅkaḷilum (Observatory) vāṉārāycci niliyaṅkaḷilum amarttikkoḷvatu, araciyalārkkut takum.
(7) maṇampaṟṟiya ceytikaḷit tiṭṭamāy muṭivu ceytukoḷḷutal 
paricam, naki, celavu, naṭimuṟi mutaliyaṉapaṟṟi, maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭārum maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭārum muṉṉamē pēcit tiṭṭamāṉa muṭivu ceytu koḷḷalvēṇṭum. allākkāl, karaṇavēḷiyilō, maṇaviruntilō kalām viḷintu, tirumaṇam niṉṟuviṭavō iṉpaṅkeṭavō nērntu viṭukiṉṟatu.  pantal naṭṭiṉāṟ paki eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
(8) tāymoḻiyiṟ karaṇam ceyvittal 
tēvamoḻiyeṉa oṉṟiṉmiyālum, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikku muntiyatum āriya moḻikaṭku mūlamum āṉatiṉālum, āriyak karaṇam tamiḻaṉukku māṉakkēṭṭi viḷippataṉālum, pirāmaṇar teṉṉāṭu varumuṉ ellāt tamiḻar maṇaṅkaḷum tamiḻilēyē naṭipeṟṟu vantatiṉālum ovvoru tamiḻaṉum tamiḻilēyē karaṇam ceyvittalvēṇṭum. pirāmaṇaṉ tamiḻaṉpaṉākit tamiḻiṟ karaṇam ceyya iciyiṉ ati ēṟṟukkoḷḷalām. tamiḻiṟ karaṇam ceyviyātavaṉ tamiḻaṉākāṉ.
ovvoru tūya tamiḻaṉum, taṉ vīṭṭuk karaṇatti maṭṭumaṉṟip piṟarvīṭṭuk karaṇattiyum tamiḻiṟ ceyvikka muyaṟci ceytal vēṇṭum. vaṭamoḻiyil naṭakkum karaṇattiṟkuc cellutal kūṭātu.
(9) vīṇcaṭaṅku vilakkal 
muṟkūṟiya vīṇ caṭaṅkukaḷōṭu, muṉṉōr kālattil nikaḻnta uṭaṉpōkku, makaṭpāṟkāñcip pōr, maṇappōṭṭip pōr mutaliyavaṟṟik kuṟikkum niṉivukūr caṭaṅkukaḷiyum vilakkal vēṇṭum.
(10) celvanilik kēṟpac celavu ceytal 
tirumaṇattil tārāḷamāyc celavu ceytal  takkatē. āyiṉ, avaravar celvanilik kēṟpac celavu ceytal vēṇṭum. puliyip pārttup pūṉi cūṭu pōṭṭukkoṇṭatupōl celvaṉip pārttu ēḻiyum celavaḻittup piṉpu kaṭaṉāl varuntuvatu naṉṟaṉṟu. kaliyāṇam ceyta vīṭṭil āṟumātam karuppu eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. cilar tirumaṇac celavāl kālamellām karuppuṇṭākkik koḷvatumuṇṭu. celvaṉ evvaḷavu celavaḻittālum atu kuṇamēyaṉṟik kuṟṟamaṉṟu. ataṉāṟ pala toḻilāḷarkkup piḻippu naṭakkum. celavu ceytal vaḻiyākavē celvaṉ celvam nāṭṭiṟ parava muṭiyum.
tirumaṇaviḻā pala nikaḻccikaḷik koṇṭatiṉāl, tiṭṭamiṭṭuc celavu ceyyiṉum ataṟkumēṟ pōyviṭum. itaṉālēyē, kaliyāṇam paṇṇippār, vīṭṭik kaṭṭippār eṉpar. celavu evvaḷavu mēṟpaṭiṉum celvaṉ ēṟṟukkoḷḷa muṭiyum; ēḻikku muṭiyātu. ākiyāl mika viḻippāyiruntu cikkaṉamāyc celavu ceytalvēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, ēḻikkum arikkācilē kaliyāṇam, atilē koñcam vāṇavēṭikki naṭakka muṭiyum. celavu ceyyap paṇamillāviṭattuc celavi niṟutta vēṇṭumē yoḻiyat tirumaṇatti niṟuttivittal kūṭātu.
āyiṉ, cila kañcar celvaniliyiruntum, aiyācāmikkuk kaliyāṇam, avaravar vīṭṭil cāppāṭu; koṭṭu muḻakkuk kōyililē, veṟṟilipākkuk kaṭiyilē eṉṟavāṟu, tirumaṇa vīṭṭiṟku vantavarkkum veṟṟili pākkuk
kūṭak koṭuppatilli. itu mikak koṭitu. maṇamakkaḷ nīṭittu nalvāḻvu vāḻvataṟku, ellār nalleṇṇamum vēṇṭiyatākum. maṇamakkaḷip piṟar vāḻttum vāḻttiṟku, maṇavīṭṭiṟ koṭukkappeṟum etuvum īṭākātu. cilar veṟṟili koṭāmi kulamarapeṉṟu pōkkuk kāṭṭuvar. aḵtuṇmiyāyiṉ, at tīya vaḻakkatti uṭaṉē māṟṟik koḷḷal vēṇṭum. iṟivaṉ tiruvaruḷ pōṉṟē makkaḷ nalleṇṇamum oruvar nalvāḻviṟku vēṇṭiyatām.
(11) maṇanāḷaṉṟē maṇamakkaḷik kūṭṭutal 
cila peṟṟōr, maṇattiṉ nōkkattiyē uṇarātu nīṇṭakālamāka iṉpantuyttu veṟutta taṅkaḷip pōṉṟē iḷamaṇamakkaḷiyuṅ karutikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. kaḷavoḻukka iṉpam vāykkāta maṇamakkaṭkellām maṇanāḷiṉpamē ciṟantatu eṉpati aṟitalvēṇṭum. mēlum, maṇanāḷiṟ kūṭāmiyāl, oḻukkakkēṭu, aiyuṟavu, matiyāmi, aṉpiṉmi, nīṭukūṭi vāḻāmi mutaliyaṉa irucārum ēṟpaṭutaṟkiṭamām.
(12) maṇamakkaḷiṉ nalvāḻvi virumpal 
cila peṟṟōr tam makaṉ marumakaḷōṭum makaḷ marumakaṉōṭum kūṭik kulavuvatiyum, ōruyirum īruṭalumāka oṉṟiyiruppatiyum virumpu vatilli. ciṟappāka, makaḷ aṅṅaṉam iruppiṉ aḻukkāṟu koṇṭu, nīṇṭakālam paḻakiya nammi maṟantuviṭṭu nēṟṟu vantavaṉoṭu kūṭik koṇṭāḷ pār eṉṟu colvatuṇṭu. aṉṉavar kīḻvarum kalittokic ceyyuṭ pakutiyik karuttūṉṟik kavaṉippārāka.
 palavuṟu naṟuñcāntam paṭuppavark kallati
maliyuḷē piṟappiṉum malikkavitām eṉceyyum
niṉiyuṅkāl nummakaḷ numakkumāṅ kaṉiyaḷē;
cīrkeḻu veṇmuttam aṇipavark kallati
nīruḷē piṟappiṉum nīrkkavitām eṉceyyum
tēruṅkāl nummakaḷ numakkumāṅ kaṉiyaḷē; 
  ēḻpuṇar iṉṉici muralpavark kallati
yāḻuḷē piṟappiṉum yāḻkkavitām eṉceyyum
cūḻuṅkāl nummakaḷ numakkumāṅ kaṉiyaḷē	(pālikkali)
itu, uṭaṉpōkkil taliviyit tēṭicceṉṟa cevilittāyi nōkki aṟiñar kūṟiyatu.
(1. uṟu-poruntum, cāntam-cantaṉam, paṭuppavar-pūcupavar, aṉiyaḷē-appaṭippaṭṭavaḷē.
2. keḻu-poruntiya, muttam-periya muttu, tēruṅkāl-ārāyumiṭattu.
3. puṇar-poruntiya, iṉṉici-iṉiya oli, muralpavar-vācippavar, yāḻ-vīṇi, cūḻuṅkāl-ārāyumiṭattu).
3. maṇamakkaḷ kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyavi 
maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷit taṉakkuc camamāṉa vāḻkkik tuṇiyākak karutalvēṇṭum; karaṇavēḷi tavira maṟṟa nērattil āciriyaṉum periyōrumpōṉṟa kuravarikkaṇṭāl, uṭaṉē eḻuntu vaṇakkañ ceytal vēṇṭum; tāṉ araca niliyil iruppatākak karutikkoṇṭu, navāpu ōlakkam (tarpār) naṭāttutal kūṭātu. maṇappantiyil tāṉum cērntu parimāṟalām, maṇamakaḷoṭu kalantu pēcalām.
maṇamakaḷ karaṇavēḷiyilum maṇamēṭiyilum piṭarivalikkat tali kaviḻntēyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatilli. maṟṟa vēḷiyilum, pēcā maṭantiyāyiruttal kūṭātu. maṟṟap peṇṭiruṭaṉ kalanturiyāṭalām; avarkku vēṇṭuvaṉa koṭuttutavalām; maṇamakaṉuṭaṉ pēcalām.
maṇamakkaḷ, maṇaviḻāviṉpiṉ kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyumāyc ceyya vēṇṭiya karumaṅkaḷum kaṭamikaḷum, eṉ maṇavāḻkkieṉṉum nūlil virivāyk kūṟappeṟum.
4. uṟṟār uṟaviṉar kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyavi 
uṟaviṉarum naṇparum, maṇamakkaḷ ēḻiyarāyum tiruppic ceyya iyalātavarāyum iruntāltāṉ avarkkup paricum  naṉkoṭiyum vaḻaṅkal vēṇṭum; celvarāyiruppiṉ tēviyilli; peruñ celvarāyiruppiṉ vaḻaṅkavē kūṭātu. avarkku vaḻaṅkuvatu poruḷiyal nūlukku māṟāṉa peruṅ kuṟṟam.
uṟaviṉar moy vikkumpōtu, maṇavīṭṭār tamakku muṉpu ceyta aḷavē ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟu karutavēṇṭuvatilli. tam celva nilikkēṟpak kūṭṭiyō kuṟittō ceyyalām; allākkāl atu vaṭṭiyillāk kaṭaṉpōliruntu taṉ ciṟappi yiḻakkum. maṇavīṭṭārum uṟaviṉar niliyaṟintu peruntaṉmiyāy iruntukoḷḷavēṇṭum.
cwÉd® maÿÇÈUªJ xU kzå£o‰F tªâU¥ã‹, fuz« Koªjã‹, v¤Jiz ÉiuªJ âU«g Koínkh m¤Jiz ÉiuªJ âU«ãÉlnt©L«; ‘v‹W bršthnuh! eṉṟu maṇavīṭṭār ēṅkik kalaṅkumāṟu, paṉṉāḷ taṅkiviṭutal kūṭātu. 
cīr ceytal, varici vittal, moyyeḻututal, paricaḷittal, naṉkoṭi vaḻaṅkal, mutaliyavi kimmāṟu karutic ceyyappaṭumpōtu, avaṟṟiṉ ēṟṟattāḻvu iḻitakavāyc collik kāṭṭappaṭutalālum, ataṉāl kaṇṇaṉṉa kēḷirkkum maṉakkacappuṇṭāvatālum, kimmāṟu karutā vakiyil avaṟṟi aṉpaḷippāka vittukkoḷvatē cālac ciṟantatām.
5. araciyalār kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyavi 
ōr uṭampu nalamāyiruttalvēṇṭiṉ, ataṉ uṟuppukaḷellām nalamāyum oṉṟupaṭṭumiruttal vēṇṭuvatupōla; oru nāṭu muṉṉēṟa vēṇṭumāyiṉ, ataṉ makkaḷ vakuppārellām muṉṉēṟiyum oṉṟupaṭṭu miruttal vēṇṭum. tamiḻnāṭṭil ittakiya muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkuk kulap piriviṉi perun taṭiyāyiruttaliṉ, araciyalār ati aṟavē oḻittaṟ kāṉa ceyalkaḷi uṭaṉaṭiyāy mēṟkoḷḷavēṇṭum. avi,
(1)	kalappumaṇañ ceyvārkku, maṇaccelavu muḻuvatiyumēṉum atil oru pakutiyiyēṉum ēṟṟukkoḷḷutalum,
(2)	avarkku maṇa muṭintavuṭaṉ avar takutikkēṟpa araciyal aluvalaḷittalum,
(3)	avar peṟum makkaṭkup paṭipputavi ceytalum ākum.
kalappumaṇamum, maṇamakaḷi nōkki, (1) uyar kalappumaṇam, (2) otta kalappumaṇam, (3) tāḻ kalappumaṇam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. oru maṟavaṉ, oru vēḷāḷap peṇṇi maṇappatu uyar kalappumaṇam; ōr iṭippeṇṇi maṇappatu otta kalappumaṇam; oru tāḻttappaṭṭa kulappeṇṇi maṇappatu tāḻ kalappumaṇam. immūṉṟum kalappuvakiyil muṟiyē kaṭiyiṭi taliyām. ātalāl, avaṟṟiṉ tarattiṟkēṟpap pārāṭṭutaviyumiruttal vēṇṭum.
oṭṭumaraṅkaḷ uyarnta kaṉikaḷit taralpōl kalappu maṇamakkaḷ aṟivāṟṟaliṟ ciṟanta makkaḷip peṟuvārātalāl, nāṭṭu muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkēṟṟa naṉmakkaṭpēṟṟi viḷippatu, araciyalār mēṟkoḷḷavēṇṭiya kaṭamiyākum.
6. putiyaṉa pukutal 
maṇamakkaḷi muṉṉamē paḻakac ceytal, acciṭṭa aḻippitaḻ viṭuttaṉṟu, potuk kaṭṭaṭattil maṇam naṭattal, maṇamakkaḷ eḷiya uṭiyuṭuttal, karaṇanaṭappik karaṇa āciriyar (purōkitaṉ) poṟuppil kuttakiyāka viṭṭuviṭal, maṇavaṟiyiṉmi, coṟpoḻivāṟṟuvittal, piyil tēṅkāypaḻaveṟṟili koṭuttal, paricēṟkāmi, ūrvalamiṉmi, kaṭṭuccōṟṟut tirumaṇam, irumaṇak kūṭṭuc celavu, tokutimaṇam, pativumaṇam mutaliyaṉa ikkālattup pōṟṟattakka putiya vaḻakkaṅkaḷām.
7. pōlic cīrtirutta maṇaṅkaḷ 
cilar kulampārttē maṇantukoṇṭum, peṇvīṭṭil muṉṉamē pirāmaṇap purōkitaṉik koṇṭu vaṭamoḻik karaṇam ceyvittuviṭṭu, piṉpu maṇamakaṉvīṭṭil tamiḻkkaraṇam naṭattikkoṇṭum muḻutta (mukūrtta) nāḷil muḻutta vēḷiyilēyē tāli kaṭṭikkoṇṭum, muṉṉamē tāli kaṭṭiviṭṭup piṉpu mōtiram aṇivittukkoṇṭum, kuttuviḷakkiṟkup patilāka miṉcāra viḷakkup pōṭṭukkoṇṭum, maṇattiṟkeṉṟu matam māṟik koṇṭum, tam maṇaṅkaḷic cīrtirutta maṇaṅkaḷeṉṟu kūṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭi nīkkit tamiḻil naṭattiya aḷavilēyē cīrtirutta maṇam ākiviṭātu. maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭār cīrtirutta maṇattiṟku iciyāmiyālō, maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭārkku atil muḻunampikki illāmiyālō, vaṭamoḻik karaṇam muṉṉamē naṭantuviṭukiṉṟatu. atōṭu niṟuttikkoḷvatu nallatu. aṅṅaṉamiṉṟit tamiḻkkaraṇamum      ceytu makkaḷi ēmāṟṟuvatu, tiruntā maṇattiṉun tīya ceyalākum. vaṭamoḻiyiṉṟit tamiḻkkaraṇam maṭṭum naṭappiṉ ati ōraḷavu cīrtiruttam eṉṉalām.
8. peṇṭir camaṉmi (camattuvam) 
tamiḻar nākarikamum paṇpāṭum aṭintu pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷākiyum, iṉṉum peṇṭir camaṉmi ēṟpaṭātatu peritum iraṅkattakkatē. tamiḻariṭip peṇ kāppu maṟamilliyeṉṟu aṇmiyil vaṭanāṭṭā roruvar paḻittatum amivuṭiyatē.
irupāluḷḷum āṇpālē ēṟṟamāṉatu eṉṉum koḷki iṉṉum iruntuvarukiṉṟatu. cāṇ piḷḷi āṇpiḷḷi māṇpiḷḷi eṉṟu āṭavarē tammip pukaḻntukoḷvatu aḻakaṉṟu. maṇavuṟavupaṟṟi naṭakkum caṇṭikaḷil, neñcu puṇpaṭa vaciyēṟpavar peṇvīṭṭārē. peṇṇip peṟṟavaṉ pēccuk kēṭpāṉ eṉpatu paḻamoḻiyākum.
maṇamakaṉ ettuṇi maṇañceytiruppiṉum, maṟumaṇattil putu    maṇavāḷappiḷḷiyākavē matikkappaṭukiṉṟāṉ; maṇamakaḷō mutal maṇattilēyē taṉ kaṉṉittaṉmiyi iḻantavaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟāḷ.
tōḷnala muṇṭu tuṟakkap paṭṭōr
vēḷnī ruṇṭa kuṭiyō raṉṉar;
nalkunar purintu nalaṉuṇap paṭṭōr
alkunar pōkiya vūrō raṉṉar;
*	nalam - aḻaku. tuṟakkappaṭṭōr - kiviṭappaṭṭōr. vēḷ - tākattāl virumpiya. kuṭi - paṉiyōlip paṭṭi. nalkunar - aṉpuceyyuṅ kātalar. purintu - virumpi. alkunar kuṭiyiruppōr.
kūṭiṉar purintu kuṇaṉuṇap paṭṭōr
cūṭiṉa riṭṭa pūvō raṉṉar.	(pālikkali, 22)
eṉṟu īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu pāṭappaṭṭa nilimi iṉṟu muḷḷa
teṉiṉ, eṉ colvatu? peṇṭir nukarccipporuḷ nilimiyiṉiṉṟu vāḻkkittuṇi nilimikku iṉṉum muṟṟum uyarttappaṭavilli eṉpatē itaṉāl aṟiyappaṭum uṇmiyām.
iṉi, peṇṭiriṉ uṟuppiyalpaṟṟiyum, maccamaṟuppaṟṟiyum, piṟappu varici paṟṟiyum, nallavun tīyavumāka ettuṇiyō colavaṭikaḷum paḻamoḻi
kaḷum uḷa. attakiya vitappīṭukaḷ āṭavarippaṟṟi mikutiyāyilli. iruppaṉavum perumpālum nallaviyē.
ivvāṟu peṇpālippaṟṟi niṟkum iḻivellām, maṇamakaḷiṉ camaṉmikkut taṭiyāyiruttaliṉ, avaṟṟit tiruttikkoḷvatu tamiḻar kaṭaṉ. āyiṉ, peṇṇiṉ peṇmipaṟṟiyum meṉmipaṟṟiyum kaṟpukkāva lāka ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa ellāk kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷum, peṇṇalattiṟkē yātaliṉ, avi eṉṟum iruntē tīralvēṇṭum. kaṇavaṉārum maṉiviyārum oruvarōṭoruvar uriyāṭumpōtu, kaṇavaṉār orumiyilum maṉiviyār uyarvup paṉmiyilum pēciṉālum, paṭarkkiyil iruvarum matippākavē pēcutal vēṇṭum. kūlivēli ceyyum kallā makkaḷāyiṉ, iruvarum orumiyiṟ pēcikkoḷḷalām.
maṉiviyār kaṇavaṉāriṉ peyarik kuṟippiṭumpōtu matippoṭu kuṟippiṭiṉ kuṟṟamākātu.
maṅkalam eṅkaṇun taṅkuka!

3. tirumaṇat tamiḻk karaṇam 
tamiḻppulavar, āciriyar, tamiḻappārppār, kulattalivar, aṟiñar, mutiyōr mutaliyavaruḷ oruvarāṉa karaṇa āciriyar allatu tirumaṇa āciriyar, tirumaṇap pantalil allatu koṭṭakiyil uḷḷa maṇavaṟi yilāyiṉum maṇamēṭiyilāyiṉum, tirumaṇa maṇṭapattil allatu kaṭṭaṭattil oru kōṭiyilāyiṉum, maṇamakkaḷi ōr aṟukāliyil (bench) allatu iṇiyirukki metti nāṟkāliyil neruṅkiyirukkac ceytu, tirumaṇa aviyōri nōkki niṉṟu,
perumāṉarē! perumāṭṭiyarē!
(allatu)
periyōrē! tāymārē!
(allatu)
uṭaṉpiṟappāḷarē! uṭaṉpiṟappāṭṭiyarē!
eṉa viḷittu,
iṉṟu......................................................... eṉṉum maṇamakaṉukkum,.................................................. eṉṉum maṇamakaḷukkum, iṟivaṉ tirumuṉpum iṅkuḷḷa periyōr muṉṉiliyilum,  (iṅkuḷḷa periyōr muṉṉiliyil) tirumaṇak karaṇam nikaḻavirukkiṉṟatu. aṉivarum amitiyāyirukkak kēṭṭukkoḷ kiṉṟēṉ; eṉṟu aviyamartti,
akara mutala eḻuttellām āti
pakavaṉ mutaṟṟē yulaku
eṉṉum mutaṟ kuṟaḷiyēṉum,
ulakam yāviyum tāmuḷa vākkalum
nilipe ṟuttalum nīkkalum nīṅkalā
alaki lāviḷi yāṭṭuṭi yāravar
talivar aṉṉavark kēcaraṇ nāṅkaḷ
eṉṉum kampavirāmāyaṇak kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkac ceyyuḷiyēṉum,
ulake lāmuṇarn tōtaṟ kariyavaṉ
nilavu lāviya nīrmali vēṇiyaṉ
alakil cōtiyaṉ ampalat tāṭuvāṉ
malarci lampaṭi vāḻtti vaṇaṅkuvām
eṉṉum periyapurāṇak kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkac ceyyuḷiyēṉum,
tuṅka illaṟat tuṇimiyi nāṭiyē
iṅka marntuḷa immaṇa makkaḷi
maṅka lammiku maṇaviṉip paṭuttavē
eṅkun taṅkiya iṟiyaṭi paṇikuvām
eṉṉum tirumaṇa iṟivaṇakkac ceyyuḷiyēṉum, pattiyuṇarcciyuṭaṉ ōti, piṉvarumāṟu karaṇam naṭattivittal vēṇṭum.
kaṭavuḷ nampikkiyillāviṭattil kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkatti viṭṭu viṭalām.

4. karaṇattoṭarpāṉa cila cīrtiruttak karuttukaḷ 
(1) piḷḷiyār vaṇakkam
piḷḷiyār vaṇakkam kaṭiccaṅka kālattiṟkup piṉputāṉ tamiḻakattiṟ pukuntatu. iṟivaṉ ōṅkāra vaṭiviṉaṉ eṉṟu collap paṭṭatiṉālum, ōṅkārattiṉ varivaṭivam yāṉivaṭivi ottiruppatālum, yāṉi vaṭivil oru teyvam āriyap pūcāriyarāl putitāyp paṭikkappaṭṭu, civaneṟiyi muṉṉiṉum mikutiyāy āriyappaṭuttavum cēyōṉ eṉṉum murukaṉukkuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭṭiyum avaṉukkut tantiyākac collap
paṭṭa civaṉukkuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭṭiyum kuṟikkavum, tamiḻarukkuḷ maṟṟumoru matappirivi yuṇṭupaṇṇavum, pukuttappaṭṭatāka aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. civaṉ allatu tirumāl eṉṉum peyarāl ellām valla iṟivaṉ ākiya muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷē vaṇaṅkappaṭutaliṉ, avaṉi vaṇaṅkuvārkku, avaṉukku makaṉ allatu marukaṉ  muṟippaṭṭa vēṟoru ciṟuteyvam vēṇṭiyatēyilli. mēlum, iṟivaṉ paṭippil taliciṟanta māntaṉ vaṭivil iṟivaṉi vaṇaṅkuvatē pakuttaṟivuḷḷa makkaṭku iḻukkāyirukka, ōr aḵṟiṇi yuyiriyiṉ vaṭivil et teyvattiyum vaṇaṅkuvatu, iv irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu uyartiṇiyāḷaṉukku eḷḷaḷavum poruntāteṉpatu collāmalē peṟappaṭum.
civaneṟiyum mālneṟiyum tamiḻa matam eṉpatum, kaṇapati vaṇakkamāṉa kāṇapattiyam piṟkāla āriyac cērkki eṉpatum, eṉ tamiḻar matam eṉṉum nūlil virivākak kūṟappeṟum.
(2) viḷakkuvittal 
nākarikam muṟṟāta paṇṭikkālattil, aimpūtaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa  tī iṟivaṉ aṭiyāḷamākak karutappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl, maṇavaṟiyil kuttuviḷakkik koḷuttivittaṉar. aṟivu mikka ikkālattiṟku ak karuttu ēṟkātu. ataṉāl, tālittaṭṭiṟ cūṭaṅkoḷuttavum vēṇṭuvatilli.
(3) aṟukiṭal 
ciṟupiḷḷi niliyilirunta mutupaṇṭimakkaḷ maṇamakkaḷi vāḻttiyapōtu, avar kuṭumpam aṟukupōl vērūṉṟi uṇavut taṭṭillāmal vāḻavēṇṭumeṉṟu kuṟittaṟku, aṟukum ariciyum kalantu avarmītu tūviṉar. iṉṟu ap poruḷkaḷiṉṟiyē ak kuṟippoṭu vāḻtta muṭiyumātaliṉ, avaṟṟit tūvavēṇṭuvatilli. vīṇāka vāriyiṟikkum ariciyi irappōrkkum ēḻimakkaṭkum aḷippiṉ, ettuṇiyō aṟappayaṉuṇṭām.
iṅku aricikkuk kūṟiyatu, maṇavaṟiyil vikkappaṭum ēṉi maṅkalap poruḷkaṭkum okkum.
5. tirumaṇa vāḻttitaḻ 
pōliki 1
iṟivaṉ vakutta iyaṟki neṟippaṭi, illaṟameṉṉum nallaṟam pukunta naṇparkāḷ,
nīṅkaḷ iṉṟē uṇmiyāṉa ulaka vāḻkki toṭaṅki, nīṅkāta vāḻkkik kūṭṭāḷiyarāyiṉīr. ōruyirum īruṭalumāka oṉṟiya kātalarāy, iṉpattilum tuṉpattilum vāḻvilum tāḻvilum orunilippaṭṭa uḷḷattiṉarāy,
aṉpum aṟaṉum uṭittāyiṉ ilvāḻkki
paṇpum payaṉum atu
eṉa vaḷḷuvar vakutta vāḻkkiyiṉarāy, aṟivuṭi makkaḷi aḷavākap peṟṟu, aḷavil kālam iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntiruppīrāka.
pōliki 2
māṇpumikka maṉiyaṟam pukka maṇamakkāḷ,
nīṅkaḷiruvīrum, aṉṟilippōla akalātiruntu, iṉpiṉukkelli immiyiṟ kaṇṭu, palvakic celvamum pāṅkāyutava, vaḷavaṉum vāḻvaraciyumāy, kāviri maṇaliṉuṅ kaḻipalanāḷ vāḻntirukka.
uṅkaḷ marapu, vāḻiyaṭi vāḻiyāy vaḻivaḻi ciṟakka.
pōliki 3
maṉṟal vāḻttumaṭal
illaṟam eṉṉum iṇivāḻkkiyēṟṟa tuṇivarkāḷ,
nīṅkaḷ iruvīrum, kātal eṉṉum pūṅkāvil iṉpam eṉṉum tēṉi nukarum vaṇṭukaḷ. illaṟam eṉṉum cakaṭatti ulakavāḻkki eṉṉum karaṭṭuppātiyil iḻuttuc cellum iḷaṅkāḷikaḷ. ñālam (pūmi) eṉṉum paṇṇiyil makkaḷ eṉṉum payiri vaḷarkkum uḻavarkaḷ.
paciyum piṇiyum pāril nīṅkavum, makkaḷ yāvarum makkaḷāy vāḻavum, uṅkaḷ vāḻkki payaṉpaṭuvatāka.
pāl puḷittālum pakal iruṇṭālum, ulakam peyarntālum uyarntōr piḻi ceytālum, nīṅkaḷ nilipiṟaḻātu nīṭūḻi vāḻntirukka.

6. tālikaṭṭum vaḻakkam tamiḻaratē 
oru kātalaṉ taṉ kātalikku, allatu oru maṇamakaṉ taṉ maṇamaka ḷukku, allatu kaṇavaṉ taṉ maṉivikku, kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭuvataṉ vāyilāy, avaḷit taṉ vāḻkkittuṇivi eṉṟu piṟarkkuk kāṭṭuvatu, tamiḻakattut toṉṟutoṭṭa vaḻakkamā yiruntuvarukiṉṟatu.
kaṭṭutal eṉṉum col maṇattal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu, tālikaṭṭum vaḻakkam paṟṟiyē. X® ïisahid mšyJ ïisahis neh¡», ‘Ú ahiu¡ f£l¥ngh»whŒ? eṉṟu kēṭpatu ulaka vaḻakku. maṇamakkaḷ iruvaruḷḷum tāli kaṭṭuvatu maṇamakaṉum avaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭuvatu maṇamakaḷumā yiruppiṉum, kaṭṭutal eṉṉum col maṇattal eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkat talippaṭṭapiṉ, atu iruvarkkum potuvāṉa collāyiṟṟu.
mutaṟkālattil, makkaḷ kuṭumpappiriviṉṟik kulaṅkulamāy allatu tokutitokutiyāy vāḻntuvanta niliyil, oru kulattup peṇṭir, perumpālum kulattalivaṉ maṉiviyar nīṅkalāka, ak kulattu āṭavar aṉivarkkum potu maṉiviyarākavē iruntuvantaṉar. piṉpu nākarikan tōṉṟit taṉimaṉiviyar ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, oruvaṉ taṉ maṉiviyi allatu maṉiviyari vēṟākap pirittu vittaṟku, tālikaṭṭum vaḻakkam ēṟpaṭṭatu. īki yariya iḻiyaṇi makaḷir (puṟam. 127),eṉpatu tamiḻappeṇṭir tāliyaṇiyum vaḻakkattik kuṟikkum caṅkanūṟ cāṉṟām.
muntukālattil, ciṟappākak kuṟiñcinilattil vāḻntu vantapōtu, iliyiyum pūviyum āṭiyaṇiyāka aṇivatu tamiḻar vaḻakkam. nālvaki yiliyuḷḷum ōliyeṉpatu uṟutiyāṉatātaliṉ, paṉaṅkuruttāl kātaṇi kaḻuttaṇi mutaliya palvēṟu aṇikaḷi avar ceytukoṇṭaṉar. cēraṉ paṉampū māliyi aṭiyāḷa māliyākak koṇṭiruntatiṉālum, ōliyik kuṟikkum tōṭu eṉṉum col iṉṟum kātaṇippeyarāy vaḻaṅkutalālum, kātil ōliyaṇivatāl ōlippaḷḷi yeṉṟu peyar peṟṟa oru paḷḷi vakuppār iṉṟiruttalālum, tāḻttappaṭṭa cila kulattup peṇṭir ōliyiyē kātil aṇivatālum, paḻaṅkālattil ōliyaṇikaḷik kātilum kaḻuttilum tamiḻap peṇṭir aṇintaṉar eṉpatil, eḷḷaḷavum viyappiṟ
kiṭamiṉṟām. kātiṟpōṉṟē kaḻuttilum paṉaṅkuruttaṇi aṇiyappaṭṭami.
tālik koḻuntit taṭaṅkaḻuttiṟ pūṇṭu
eṉṉum nālāyirat teyvap paṉuvalaṭiyāl (tiv. periyāḻ. 2, 6, 1) teriyavarum.
tālattiṉ ōliyiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatiṉālō, tālpōl toṅkuvatiṉālō, peṇṭiriṉ tirumaṇak kaḻuttaṇi tāliyeṉap peyar peṟṟirukkalām. tālam paṉi. tāl-nāvu. iṉi, nāli (toṅkuvatu) eṉpatu tāli eṉat tirintatu eṉalumām.
makkaḷ nākarikamaṭintu poṉṉāl aṇi ceyyat toṭaṅkiyapiṉ, tāliyum poṉṉāṟ ceyyappeṟṟatu.
poṟṟāli yōṭeviyum pōm
eṉṟār auviyār.
cilar poṟṟāliyil maṇiyum patittukkoṇṭaṉar.
nāṇuḷ ḷiṭṭuc cuṭarvīcu naṉmāṇikka nakutāli
pēṇi nallār kaḻuttaṇintu
eṉpatu cintāmaṇi (2697).
paṉmaṇip pūṇuñ ciṉmaṇit tāliyum
eṉpatu peruṅkati (19:119)
paṇṭik kālattil kaḻutti laṇiyappaṭum aṇikaḷellām tāliyeṉappaṭṭami, accuttāli muḷittāli pulippaṟṟāli aimpaṭittāli mutaliya peyarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum. accuttāli eṉpatu kācumāli. muḷittāli yeṉpatu ciṟumiyar kaḻuttilaṇiyap peṟum ciṟumaṇimāli. pulippaṟṟāli yeṉpatu kuṟiñcinilac ciṟār kaḻuttilum peṇṭir kaḻuttilum aṇiyappeṟum pulippal māli. aimpaṭittāli eṉpatu tirumāliṉ aimpaṭiyākiya caṅku cakkara vilvāḷ taṇṭavaṭiviṟ ceyyappaṭṭu, ciṟuvar kaḻuttil pātukāppāka aṇiyappeṟuvatu. aimpaṭittāli, 77ām puṟappāṭṭil tāliyeṉṟē collappaṭṭatu.
iṉi, kutiriyiṉ kaḻuttil aṇiyappeṟṟa tāliyeṉṉum aṇiyum uṇṭu.
valiyuṭi yurattiṉ vāṉpoṟ ṟāli	(18:13)
eṉṟu peruṅkati kūṟutal kāṇka. cila veḷḷāṭṭiṉ kaḻuttil toṅkum catiyum, oppumipaṟṟit tāliyeṉappaṭum.
kayiṟkaṭi yoḻukiya kāmar tūmaṇi
ceyattaku kōvi	(6 : 01 - 2)
eṉṉum cilappatikārat toṭaril, kōvi eṉpataṟkup piṉṟāli yeṉṟu arumpatavurikārarum aṭiyārkku nallārum urittiruppatāl, akkālattu muṉṟāli piṉṟāliyeṉa iruvaki yaṇi yiruntamiyum peṟappaṭum. muṉṟāli mārpil toṅkuvatu; piṉṟāli mutukil toṅkuvatu. ivaṟṟāl, tāli eṉa oru taṉiyaṇi muṟkālattiruntamiyum, piṉpu eḻunta ataṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷ avaṟṟiṟkēṟpa vevvēṟu aṭiyaṭuttuk kūṟappaṭṭamiyum, peṟappaṭum.
tāli eṉa oru kuṟippiṭṭa maṅkala aṇi piṟkālattut tōṉṟiyapiṉ, atu ovvoru kulattiṟkum oṉṟāka vevvēṟu vaṭivilum aḷavilum ceyyappaṭṭu atataṟkēṟpap peyarum peṟṟatu. āmittāli, poṭṭuttāli, ciṟutāli, peruntāli eṉpaṉa cila peyarkaḷ.
tālipōṉṟē tālikkoṭiyum maṅkalamākak karutappaṭṭu, maṅkalanāṇ eṉappeṟṟatu. atu mutaṟkaṇ mañcaḷ tōytta nūṟkayiṟā yiruntatiṉāl, cila kulattār iṉṉum ataṉ paḻaniliyi marapākap pōṟṟik kāppar. cila kulattār atip poṟkoṭiyākac ceytukoḷvar. iṉi, maṅkalanāṇiyē tāliyākak koṇṭārum uṇṭu.
tāliyiyum, taṉiyāka aṇivatum vēṟu cila urukkaḷuṭaṉ cērttu aṇivatumāka, iruvēṟu marapukaḷ eḻuntuḷḷaṉa. tāliyiyē aṇiyāka mutupaṇṭi niliyil, iṉṟum pala anākarikat tiraviṭa marapiṉar uḷar. koṅku
nāṭṭu vēḷāḷap peṇṭir kaṉatta tāliyi aṇivatāl, ciṟappu nāḷkaḷil ati aṇivatum piṟa nāḷkaḷiṟ kaḻaṟṟi vittiruppatum avar vaḻakkam.
tālikaṭṭuvatu āriyar vaḻakkamaṉṟeṉpatu, piṉvarum (P.T.) cīnivāca aiyaṅkār kūṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
tirumaṇac caṭaṅkukaḷil, talimiyāṉatākat tāliyaṇivatum,   mañcaḷ tōytta nāṇik kaḻuttiṟ kaṭṭuvatum pōṉṟa cila aṉāriya vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, teṟkattup pirāmaṇappeṇṭirkku arumiyāka uḷḷaṉa tamiḻar varalāṟu (History of the Tamils) pa. 57.
itu (tāliyaṇivatu) kirukiya cūttiraṅkaḷilēyē collappaṭāta oru tūya tamiḻa vaḻakkam. kiyip piṭikkum pāṇik  kirakaṇattiyum ēḻaṭiyiṭum capta patīyiyumē, tirumaṇac caṭaṅkiṉ uyirnāṭip pakutikaḷākak kirukiya cūttiraṅkaḷ koḷkiṉṟaṉa mēṟpaṭi, aṭikkuṟippu.
pāṇikkirakaṇam eṉpatu eṅṅaṉam āriyarkku mutaṉmiyāṉatō, aṅṅaṉamē tālikaṭṭu eṉpatum tamiḻarkku mutaṉmiyāṉa teṉka.
maṇamakaṉ tāliyi eṭuttu, peṇṇē! aṟappayaṉ peṟuvataṟkut tāli kaṭṭukiṉṟēṉ eṉṟu colli, maṇamakaḷ kaḻuttil oru kayiṟṟāṟ kaṭṭukiṉṟāṉ. ik kūṟṟu vētamantiramaṉṟu. maṇaccaṭaṅkiṉ ip pakutiyum kirukiya cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ collappaṭavilli. teṉṉāṭṭuk kulamarapu (Castes and Tribes of Southern India), pa. 285.
kaḻuttilaṇiyum tāliyiṉum kiyilaṇiyum mōtiramē kaṇṇiyamum nākarikamum vāyntateṉiṉ, kottukkottāyp poṟṟoṭariyum, motta mottamāyp paṉmaṇimāliyum kaḻuttilaṇiyumpōtu, tālikkēṉ iṭamilliyeṉa etir viṉavi viṭukka.
kuṟippu : tamiḻarellārum ōriṉamāyiṉum, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷil vēṟupaṭṭa palvēṟu tamiḻak kulaṅkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntuvaruvatālum, kaṭikkaḻakak kālattilēyē cila tamiḻak kulaṅkaḷ cila āriya vaḻakkaṅkaḷi mēṟkoṇṭuviṭṭataṉālum, itupōtuḷḷa ōriru cāṉṟukaḷimaṭṭuṅ koṇṭu, kaḻakakkālattil tāli oru kulattārkkum maṅkalavaṇiyākavilli eṉṟu muḻuvuṟutiyāyk kūṟiviṭa muṭiyātu. āyiṉ, atu mutaṟkālattil aḻaku allatu kāppuppaṟṟiya potuvaṇiyākavē tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉpatum, atu tamiḻar aṇiyē eṉpatum teḷḷat teḷivām.
7. maliyāḷa nāṭṭu maṇamuṟi 
paṇṭic cēranāṭākiya maliyāḷa nāṭṭil, āriyat toṭarpāl, maṇamuṟiyil mikunta māṟutalkaḷ nērntuḷḷaṉa. cēranāṭṭil mutaṉmutaṟ kuṭipukunta pirāmaṇa vakuppārāṉa nampūtirimār tammi nilattēvar (pūcurar) eṉa nāṭṭi matat tuṟiyil aḷaviṟanta teyvikac celvākkup peṟṟatuṭaṉ, āṅkāṅkup perunilakkiḻārum kuṟunila maṉṉarumāy āṭcittuṟiyilum mikunta atikārattik kippaṟṟi, kulaviyal variciyil tammit taliyākac ceytu, cēranāṭṭup paḻantamiḻk kuṭikaḷuṭ ciṟanta nāyarkulap peṇṭiri, tam viruppampōl poṟuppaṟṟa muṟiyil nukartaṟkēṟṟa maṇamuṟikaḷi vakuttuviṭṭaṉar.
nampūtirip pirāmaṇarkkuḷ pala makkaḷiruppiṉ taliccaṉukkē allatu mūttavaṉukkē cotturimiyum maṇavurimiyumātalāl, avaṉukkiḷi yavarellām nāyattimāriyē vippu muṟiyil maṉiviyarāka āṇṭu vantaṉar. āyiṉ, avarkkup piṟakkum piḷḷikaṭkut tantiyiṉ cotturimi yillātatāl marumakkaṭṭāyam (ammāṉ cotturimi) nāyar kulattil ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. pirāmaṇar entac camiyattilum enta nāyattiyiyum nukarumāṟu palkaṇavamum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. nāyattimār nāyariyum pirāmaṇariyum maṭṭum maṇakkalāmeṉṟum, avaruḷ pirāmaṇari maṇappatu teyvatti maṇappatu pōṉṟa perumpēṟu eṉṟum, ataṉāl kulam uyarumeṉṟum, karuttukaḷ eḻuntaṉa. pirāmaṇar, nāyattimāroṭu kāmanukarcciyaṉṟi vēṟevvaki uṟavuṅkoḷḷātu, avarit tam illattilum cērkkātu, tām vēṟākavē vāḻntu vantaṉar. avarukku nāyattimāriṭam piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷum, tam tantiyari aṟiyātu tam tāyāruṭaṉ tari eṉṟum tarivāṭu eṉṟum collappaṭum nāyar kuṭiyiruppukaḷil nāyar kulattiṉarāka vaḷarntu vantaṉar.
maṇavātu kaṉṉiyāy iṟappavaḷ tīkkatiyaṭivāḷ eṉṟoru mūṭanampikki nampūtiriyarāṟ pukuttappaṭṭuviṭṭatiṉāl, mutalāvatu nāyar kulattilum piṉpu ataṉiṉun tāḻnta vēṟu cila kulaṅkaḷilum, tālikaṭṭu eṉṉum pakaṭikkūttāṉa iḷami maṇam ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, peṇvīṭṭārkkuc celavum, pirāmaṇaruḷḷiṭṭa piṟarkku varavum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. tāli eṉṉum maṅkala aṇi taṉ ciṟappiyalpi iḻantatu. makaḷir pūppaṭiyumuṉ ceyyappaṭum tālikaṭṭu eṉṉum maṇaviṉi nāḷaṭivil poruḷaṟṟup pōyviṭṭataṉāl, avar pūppaṭinta piṉ mīṇṭum oru maṇaviṉi (āyiṉ, uṇmiyāṉa tirumaṇam) naṭipeṟalāyiṟṟu. itu campantam eṉṉum vaṭacoṟ peyarāl vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu. ï§‡d« kzÉid ïu©lhÆ‰W.*
tālikaṭṭu
tālikaṭṭu eṉṉum maṇaviṉiyippaṟṟi, tiru (K.R.) kiruṭṭiṇamēṉaṉ kūṟiya cāṉṟiyamāka 1894ām āṭṭi malapār maṇaviṭik kuḻu (Malabar Marriage Commission) aṟikkiyil, varintiruppatāvatu:
tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam eṉpatu, oruvakiyil, maṟṟa māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil oru tēvakaṇiki (tēvatāci) avaḷ toḻilit toṭaṅkumuṉ, avaṭkuc ceyyappaṭum caṭaṅki ottatākum. aracakuṭumpaṅkaḷilum cila iṭip
pirapukkaḷ kuṭumpaṅkaḷilum oru cattiriyaṉ, allatu caraṇappirivil oru neṭuṅkāṭi, kuṟikkappaṭṭa maṅkalavēḷiyil peṇvīṭṭiṟku aḻikkap paṭṭuvantu, naṇpar muṉpum kulattār muṉpum peṇkaḻuttil tāliyik kaṭṭiviṭṭu, ataṟkuriya kūliyi vāṅkikkoṇṭu pōyviṭukiṉṟāṉ. maṟṟap pirivukaḷil, peṇṇiṉ piṟappiyam (cātakam) avaḷuṭiya iṉaṅkaṉ kuṭumpattup piyaṉkaḷiṉ piṟappiyaṅkaḷōṭu oppunōkkappaṭṭu, otta piṟappiyattiṟkuriya piyaṉ tālikaṭṭat takuntavaṉākak kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. tālikaṭṭak kaṇiyaṉāl orunāḷ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. piyaṉiṉ kuṭumpattuk karaṇavaṉukkuc ceytiyit terivikkiṉṟaṉar. piyaṉukku ayiṉiyūṇ eṉṟoru viruntu aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. atiliruntu avaṉ maṇavāḷaṉ allatu piḷḷi eṉa aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. maṇavāḷaṉukku viruntu naṭanta vīṭṭiliruntu ōr ūrvalam puṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. vāḷum kēṭakamum tāṅkiya āṭavar, oruvakip pōrkkūccaliṭṭukkoṇṭu ūrvalattiṉ muṉ celkiṉṟaṉar. intac camiyattil peṇvīṭṭiliruntum ittakiya āṭavaroṭum ittakiya kūccaloṭum ōr ūrvalam kiḷampukiṉṟatu. peṇṇiṉ taravāṭṭic cērnta oruvaṉ ūrvalattiṉ muṉvantu, maṇavāḷaṉi etirkoṇṭu peṇ
vīṭṭiṟku aḻittuc celkiṉṟāṉ. aṅkē, peṇṇiṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāṉ avaṉ pātaṅkaḷik kaḻuvi, ōr iṇi vēṭṭiyip peṟṟukkoḷkiṉṟāṉ. piṉpu maṇavāḷaṉi, mūṅkiṟpāyum camukkāḷamum, veḷḷittuṇiyum virittuḷḷa pantaliṉ naṭuviṭattiṟkuk koṇṭupōy iruttukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉ piṉ, peṇṇiṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāṉ avaḷi vīṭṭiṟkuḷḷiruntu tūkkik
koṇṭu vantu, mummuṟi pantali valamvantu maṇavāḷaṉiṉ iṭappuṟattil viṭṭuviṭukiṉṟāṉ. aṉṟu peṇṇiṉ tanti kampaḷiyiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa puttāṭikaḷi, iruvarkkum aḷikkiṉṟāṉ. iruvarum, mantiravaṭi eṉṉum ap puttāṭikaḷi aṇintu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. piṉpu, peṇṇiṉ taravāṭṭuk karaṇavaṉ maṉivi, atē kulattiṉaḷāyiruppiṉ, peṇṇic cilampu mutaliya aṇikaḷāl alaṅkarikkiṉṟāḷ. ataṉ piṉ iḷiyāttu eṉṉum oru tāḻnta pirāmaṇa vakuppic cērnta purōkitaṉ, tāliyi maṇavāḷaṉiṭam koṭukkiṉṟāṉ. kuṭumpak kaṇiyaṉ muḻuttam (mukūrttam) eṉṟu kattukiṉṟāṉ. maṇavāḷaṉ taṉ vāḷi maṭiyiliṭṭu, tāliyip peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttiṟ kaṭṭukiṉṟāṉ. aṉṟu peṇ ōr ampiyum oru mukakkaṇṇāṭiyiyum kiyiṟ piṭittukkoṇṭirukkiṉṟāḷ. celvakkuṭumpaṅkaḷil, oru pirāmaṇi maṇamakkaḷi vāḻttic cila pāṭṭup pāṭukiṉṟāḷ. avaḷi amartta muṭiyāta eḷiya kuṭumpaṅkaḷil, pāṭṭil tērcci peṟṟa oru nāyaṉ ap paṇiyi āṟṟukiṉṟāṉ. piṉpu iṉaṅkar maṇavāḷaṉiyum peṇṇiyum, vīṭṭiṟkuḷ cuvaṭikkappaṭṭa ōr aṟikkut tūkkikkoṇṭu pōkiṉṟaṉar. aṅkē avarkaḷ oruvakit tīṭṭuniliyil munnāḷik kaḻikkiṉṟaṉar. nālām nāḷ, iruvarum, arukiluḷḷa nīrniliyil, oruvar kikaḷi oruvar piṭittuk koṇṭu nīrāṭukiṉṟaṉar. nīrāṭi āṭimāṟṟiyapiṉ, ūrvalattiṉ piṉṉāka vīṭu tirumpukiṉṟaṉar. mēḷatāḷamum yāṉikaḷum ūrvalattil vaḻakkamāka vikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mañcaḷnīr teḷikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. vīṭuvantu cērumpōtu, ellāk katavukaḷum cāttappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. maṇavāḷaṉ avaṟṟi valintu taḷḷit tiṟantu, vīṭṭiṟkuḷ pukuntu vaṭakkuc ciṟakil amarkiṉṟāṉ. peṇṇiṉ attiyum tōḻimārum maṇamakkaḷi aṇuki avarkaṭkut tittippup paṇṭaṅkaḷit tarukiṉṟaṉar. peṇ maṇavāḷaṉukkuc cōṟu paṭikkiṉṟāḷ. iruvarum ōriliyil uṇṭapiṉ pantalukkuc celkiṉṟaṉar. aṅkē oru tuṇi iraṇṭākak kiḻikkappaṭṭu, oru pāti maṇavāḷaṉukkum oru pāti peṇṇiṟkumāka, iṉaṅkar muṉpum naṇpar muṉpum vēṟu vēṟu koṭukkappaṭukiṉṟatu. tuṇikiḻippu, tīrvi (Divorce) ceytuviṭṭatākak kuṟippatu pōlak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatu.
ic cāṉṟiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa parupporuṭ ceytikaḷ maliyāḷa nāṭṭiṟkellām potuvēṉum, nuṇporuṭ ceytikaḷ avvav iṭattiṟkum avaravar celva nilikkum ēṟpa vēṟupaṭṭaviyākum. maṇavāḷaṉ otta kulattāṉākavum iḷiñaṉākavumē yirukkavēṇṭu meṉpatilli; mūttavaṉākavum pirāmaṇaṉākavu mirukkalām. oruvaṉē orē camiyattilō vevvēṟu camiyattilō pala peṇkaḷukkut tāli kaṭṭalām. peṇ nālām
nāḷ tāliyik kaḻaṟṟiviṭalām. peṇṇiṟku maṇavāḷaṉoṭu potuvāyt toṭarpillātupōyiṉum, avaṉ iṟantapiṉ tīṭṭuk kaḻippatu vaḻakkam. cilaviṭattu maṇavāḷaṉē kaṇavaṉāvatu muṇṭu. an nilimi tālikaṭṭaṉṟē muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. cikkaṉattiṟkākap pattāṇṭu allatu paṉṉīrāṇṭiṟ korumuṟi, pala tālikaṭṭukaḷit tokutiyāka naṭattuvatu vaḻakkam. atuvum iyalāta eḷiya tāymār, teyvac ciliyiyō vāḷiyō maṇavāḷaṉākak koṇṭu, tāmē tam makaḷirkkut tāli kaṭṭuvatuṇṭu. patiṉōrāṇṭu nirampu
muṉ tāli kaṭṭappaṭṭuviṭum. cilar ōrāṭṭip peṇṇiṟkuk kaṭṭuvatu muṇṭu. bghJthf, jhÈf£L âUkz¤njhL bjhl®g‰w jÅ¢rl§fhfnt brŒa¥gL«.*
iṅṅaṉam itu poruttamaṟṟa caṭaṅkāyiṉum, kōvilakam eṉṉum araṇmaṉikaḷilum periya nāyar illaṅkaḷilum, āyiram pirāmaṇari uṇpippatum pattāyiram patiṉāyiram urupā paṇṭi nāḷkaḷilēyē celavaḻippatum uṇṭu.
campantam
makaḷir pūppaṭintapiṉ ceyyappaṭum uṇmiyāṉa tirumaṇamākiya campantam, viyappiṟkum aṟivēṭkikkum uriyatākāmiyiṉ, virivāka
viḷakkappaṭa vēṇṭuvataṉṟu. pirāmaṇar campantam eṉṉum orē vaṭa
colli āṇṭuvariṉum, nāyariṭiyē, puṭamuṟi allatu puṭavik koṭi, ūḻam poṟukkuki (muṟivarumvari kāttiruttal), vīṭāram kayaṟuki (maṉiviyiṉ vīṭu pukutal), kiṭakkaṟi (paḷḷiyaṟi)k kaliyāṇam, mutaliyaṉa
vāka vevvēṟu tamiḻp peyarkaḷ āṅkāṅku vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. ip peyarkaḷ iṭavēṟupāṭu maṭṭumaṉṟu, vaki vēṟupāṭum paṟṟiyaṉavākum. nāyar tirumaṇavakikaḷuḷ mikunta celavāṉatu puṭamuṟi eṉpatē. iv vakippaṭi tirumaṇam ceyta kaṇavaṉē, taṉ maṉiviyit taṉ vīṭṭiṟku aḻittuc cella muṭiyum. ēṉivakippaṭi ceytavarellām, maṉivi vīṭṭiṟkē ceṉṟu
varal vēṇṭum. mutalil vēṟuvakiyāl maṇantavaṉ piṉpu celvanili yuyarnta piṉ puṭamuṟi vakiyuñceytu taṉ maṉiviyit taṉ illattiṟku aḻittukkoḷḷalām. itu pala piḷḷikaḷ piṟantapiṉ nikaḻvatumuṇṭu.
ellāt tirumaṇa vakikaṭkum potuvāṉa nikaḻccikaḷ, maṇamakkaḷiṉ piṟappiyam pārttal, maṇavuṟutippēccu, muḻuttaṅ kuṟittal, maṇaviḻāk koṇṭāṭṭam, karaṇam, pirāmaṇarkkut tāṉam, tirumaṇaviruntu, maṇamakkaḷ kūṭṭam eṉpaṉavākum.
palkaṇavam
cērar marapu aṟṟuppōyc cēranāṭṭiṟkuc cōḻa pāṇṭi nāṭṭoṭu toṭarpu nīṅkiyatiliruntu, patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari, nāyar kulattiṟ palkaṇavam kiyāḷappaṭṭu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu.
tippu kaltāṉ, 1788ām āṇṭil, iṉṟu kaḷḷikkōṭṭi eṉat tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum kōḻikkōṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟiruntapōtu, nāyarkulap palkaṇavattik kaṇṭittup piṉvarumāṟu oru kaṭṭaḷi yaṟikki piṟappittatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.

palkaṇava muṟippaṭi, iṭikkālattil ovvoru nāyattiyum, iruvar mutal paṉṉiruvarvari pala kaṇavaroṭu kūṭi vāḻntāḷ. ovvoru kaṇavaṉum, maṇanta varicippaṭi muṟikoṇṭu, oppantattiṟkēṟpa oru nāḷō palanāḷō taṉ nāyattiyoṭu kūṭiyiruppāṉ. oru naṇpakaliliruntu maṟṟoru naṇpakalvari oru nāṭkaṇakkākum. oru kaṇavaṉ vīṭṭiṟkuḷ
